Potential file‑structure conflicts
* The new prompt titles map directly onto the existing folders under backend/WeUP.Application, backend/WeUP.Domain, backend/WeUP.Contracts, backend/WeUP.Infrastructure, and the Vue 3 front‑end (frontend‑vue/src).
* No new top‑level directories are introduced – every bundle simply populates code that already has a natural home (e.g., ingestion contracts go in WeUP.Contracts/Ingestion, OCR services in WeUP.Domain/Ocr, moderation in WeUP.Application/Moderation, map UI in frontend‑vue/src/components).
* Because the original repository already contains stub files for these concerns (e.g., IngestionOrchestrator.cs, DeduplicationService.cs, PublishEligibilityService.cs), the prompts will replace those stubs rather than create duplicate files. As long as you run the prompts against the existing files (or delete the stubs first) there will be no naming collisions.
Fit to the “MVP‑first” model
The titles below are scoped to the core MVP that the checklist (v0.10 → v0.20 → v0.30) defines:
1. Ingestion → canonical event (flyer upload → OCR → normalization → evidence)
2. Deduplication & safe merge (deterministic scoring, conflict‑aware planning)
3. Moderation & publish gate (risk scoring, immutable audit)
4. Canonical Event aggregate & versioning (single source of truth)
5. Map & discovery surface (Mapbox rendering, clustering, detail modal, time‑filter)
6. Calendar overlay & temporal engine (sync with map, “Tonight/Weekend” windows)
7. Save / user context (save/unsave, anonymous local store, migration to auth)
8. Minimal observability (core ingestion metrics, health checks)
Everything that belongs to later phases (ticketing, QR, door‑scan, sponsors, wearables, blockchain, CRA optimizer, deep social graph, etc.) is deliberately omitted, so the bundle set stays within the MVP scope.
________________

using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// The public request object accepted by the ingestion API.
/// </summary>
public sealed record IngestionRequest(
    Guid RequestId,
    IngestionSourceType SourceType,
    string RawInput,                               // URL string or base64‑encoded image data
    string? SubmittedByUserId,                     // null for anonymous submissions
    DateTimeOffset SubmittedAtUtc,
    IReadOnlyDictionary<string, string>? Metadata = null);

/// </summary>
public sealed record RawIngestionPayload(
    Guid RequestId,
    byte[] ImageBytes,
    string? SourceUrl,
    string ContentHash,               // SHA‑256 of ImageBytes – computed immediately
    DateTimeOffset ReceivedAtUtc);

📁 WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/ISourceAdapter.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/ISourceAdapter.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Abstraction for turning an <see cref="IngestionRequest"/> into a <see cref="RawIngestionPayload"/>.
/// Implementations must be side‑effect free (no DB, no OCR, no network). 
/// </summary>
public interface ISourceAdapter
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Adapt the incoming request to a payload ready for the ingestion pipeline.
    /// </summary>
    Task<RawIngestionPayload> AdaptAsync(IngestionRequest request);
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/ISourceAdapter.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------

/// <summary>
/// Abstraction for turning an <see cref="IngestionRequest"/> into a <see cref="RawIngestionPayload"/>.
/// Implementations must be side‑effect free (no DB, no OCR, no network). 
/// </summary>
public interface ISourceAdapter
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Adapt the incoming request to a payload ready for the ingestion pipeline.
    /// </summary>
    Task<RawIngestionPayload> AdaptAsync(IngestionRequest request);
}


📁 WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/SourceAdapterFactory.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/SourceAdapterFactory.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using Microsoft.Extensions.DependencyInjection;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Simple factory that resolves a concrete <see cref="ISourceAdapter"/> based on the request's <see cref="IngestionSourceType"/>.
/// </summary>
public static class SourceAdapterFactory
{
    public static ISourceAdapter Create(IngestionSourceType type, IServiceProvider services)
        => type switch
        {
            IngestionSourceType.ImageUpload => services.GetRequiredService<ImageUploadAdapter>(),
            IngestionSourceType.ImageUrl    => services.GetRequiredService<ImageUrlAdapter>(),
            IngestionSourceType.ManualEntry=> services.GetRequiredService<ManualEntryAdapter>(),
            _ => throw new NotSupportedException($"Unsupported source type: {type}")
        };
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/SourceAdapterFactory.cs

📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/Adapters/ImageUploadAdapter.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/Adapters/ImageUploadAdapter.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion.Adapters;


/// <summary>
/// Handles multipart/form‑data image uploads (base64 strings are accepted).
/// </summary>
public sealed class ImageUploadAdapter : ISourceAdapter
{
    public Task<RawIngestionPayload> AdaptAsync(IngestionRequest request)
    {
        if (request.SourceType != IngestionSourceType.ImageUpload)
            throw new ArgumentException("Request source type mismatch.");


        // Assume RawInput is a base64‑encoded PNG/JPG.
        var imageBytes = Convert.FromBase64String(request.RawInput);
        var hash = ComputeSha256(imageBytes);
        var payload = new RawIngestionPayload(
            requestId: request.RequestId,
            imageBytes: imageBytes,
            sourceUrl: null,
            contentHash: hash,
            receivedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);


        return Task.FromResult(payload);
    }


    private static string ComputeSha256(byte[] data)
    {
        using var sha = SHA256.Create();
        var hashBytes = sha.ComputeHash(data);
        return Convert.ToHexString(hashBytes);
    }
}

📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/Adapters/ImageUrlAdapter.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/Adapters/ImageUrlAdapter.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Net.Http;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion.Adapters;


/// <summary>
/// Downloads an image from a publicly reachable URL and produces a payload.
/// </summary>
public sealed class ImageUrlAdapter : ISourceAdapter
{
    private readonly HttpClient _httpClient;


    public ImageUrlAdapter(HttpClient httpClient) => _httpClient = httpClient;


    public async Task<RawIngestionPayload> AdaptAsync(IngestionRequest request)
    {
        if (request.SourceType != IngestionSourceType.ImageUrl)
            throw new ArgumentException("Request source type mismatch.");


        var response = await _httpClient.GetAsync(request.RawInput);
        response.EnsureSuccessStatusCode();


        var imageBytes = await response.Content.ReadAsByteArrayAsync();
        var hash = ComputeSha256(imageBytes);


        var payload = new RawIngestionPayload(
            requestId: request.RequestId,
            imageBytes: imageBytes,
            sourceUrl: request.RawInput,
            contentHash: hash,
            receivedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);


        return payload;
    }


    private static string ComputeSha256(byte[] data)
    {
        using var sha = SHA256.Create();
        var hashBytes = sha.ComputeHash(data);
        return Convert.ToHexString(hashBytes);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------

📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/Adapters/ManualEntryAdapter.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/Adapters/ManualEntryAdapter.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Security.Cryptography;
using System.Text;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion.Adapters;


/// <summary>
/// Handles plain‑text manual entry (e.g., a JSON payload describing a flyer).  
/// For the MVP we treat the RawInput string as a UTF‑8 encoded image representation –
/// real implementations would generate an image from the text or store the raw text as‑is.
/// </summary>
public sealed class ManualEntryAdapter : ISourceAdapter
{
    public Task<RawIngestionPayload> AdaptAsync(IngestionRequest request)
    {
        if (request.SourceType != IngestionSourceType.ManualEntry)
            throw new ArgumentException("Request source type mismatch.");


        // For simplicity – treat the raw text as UTF‑8 bytes.
        var imageBytes = Encoding.UTF8.GetBytes(request.RawInput);
        var hash = ComputeSha256(imageBytes);


        var payload = new RawIngestionPayload(
            requestId: request.RequestId,
            imageBytes: imageBytes,
            sourceUrl: null,
            contentHash: hash,
            receivedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);


        return Task.FromResult(payload);
    }


    private static string ComputeSha256(byte[] data)
    {
        using var sha = SHA256.Create();
        var hashBytes = sha.ComputeHash(data);
        return Convert.ToHexString(hashBytes);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------

What you get:
Immutable request/payload contracts (IngestionRequest, RawIngestionPayload).
Enum (IngestionSourceType).
ISourceAdapter interface and a simple factory.
Three concrete adapters ready to be registered in DI (e.g., services.AddTransient<ImageUploadAdapter>();).

2️⃣ M1‑P02 – Implement Flyer OCR Pipeline v1.0
📁 WeUP.Contracts/Ocr/OcrContracts.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Ocr/OcrContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;


/// <summary>
/// Simple rectangular bounding‑box used by OCR providers.
/// </summary>
public sealed record BoundingBox(int X, int Y, int Width, int Height);


/// <summary>
/// One piece of text extracted from the image together with its confidence and location.
/// </summary>
public sealed record OcrTextBlock(
    string Text,
    float Confidence,          // 0.0 – 1.0
    BoundingBox Box);


/// <summary>
/// Full OCR result for a single ingestion payload.
/// </summary>
public sealed record OcrResult(
    Guid RequestId,
    string RawText,                     // Complete raw OCR output – never altered
    IReadOnlyList<OcrTextBlock> Blocks, // May be empty
    float Confidence,                   // Aggregate confidence (0.0 – 1.0)
    DateTimeOffset ProcessedAtUtc);


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Ocr/OcrContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;


/// <summary>
/// Simple rectangular bounding‑box used by OCR providers.

public sealed record BoundingBox(int X, int Y, int Width, int Height);


/// <summary>
/// One piece of text extracted from the image together with its confidence and location.
/// </summary>
public sealed record OcrTextBlock(
    string Text,
    float Confidence,          // 0.0 – 1.0
    BoundingBox Box);


/// <summary>
/// Full OCR result for a single ingestion payload.
/// </summary>
public sealed record OcrResult(
    Guid RequestId,
    string RawText,                     // Complete raw OCR output – never altered
    IReadOnlyList<OcrTextBlock> Blocks, // May be empty
    float Confidence,                   // Aggregate confidence (0.0 – 1.0)
    DateTimeOffset ProcessedAtUtc);


📁 WeUP.Domain/Ocr/IOcrService.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ocr/IOcrService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Ocr;


/// <summary>
/// OCR service abstraction. Implementations may wrap any 3rd‑party provider
/// (Azure Computer Vision, Google Vision, Tesseract, etc.).
/// </summary>
public interface IOcrService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Extract text from the supplied <paramref name="payload"/>.  
    /// Must never throw for empty images – return an empty OcrResult with 0 confidence.
    /// </summary>
    Task<OcrResult> ExtractAsync(RawIngestionPayload payload);
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ocr/IOcrService.cs

    Task<OcrResult> ExtractAsync(RawIngestionPayload payload);

📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Ocr/MockOcrService.cs (dummy implementation for unit tests / early dev)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ocr/MockOcrService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;
using WeUP.Domain.Ocr;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ocr;


/// <summary>
/// Very simple deterministic mock that pretends every image contains the text
/// “MOCK EVENT – 01/01/2025 – 123 Main St”.  Used only for early integration tests.
/// </summary>
public sealed class MockOcrService : IOcrService
{
    public Task<OcrResult> ExtractAsync(RawIngestionPayload payload)
    {
        // No real image processing – deterministic output.
        var raw = "MOCK EVENT – 01/01/2025 – 123 Main St";
        var blocks = new List<OcrTextBlock>
        {
            new OcrTextBlock("MOCK EVENT", 0.95f, new BoundingBox(10, 10, 200, 30)),
            new OcrTextBlock("01/01/2025", 0.90f, new BoundingBox(10, 50, 150, 25)),
            new OcrTextBlock("123 Main St", 0.92f, new BoundingBox(10, 80, 180, 25))
        };


        var result = new OcrResult(
            RequestId: payload.RequestId,
            RawText: raw,
            Blocks: blocks,
            Confidence: 0.92f,
            ProcessedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);


        return Task.FromResult(result);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ocr/MockOcrService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;
using WeUP.Domain.Ocr;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ocr;


/// <summary>
/// Very simple deterministic mock that pretends every image contains the text
/// “MOCK EVENT – 01/01/2025 – 123 Main St”.  Used only for early integration tests.
/// </summary>
public sealed class MockOcrService : IOcrService
{
    public Task<OcrResult> ExtractAsync(RawIngestionPayload payload)
    {
        // No real image processing – deterministic output.
        var raw = "MOCK EVENT – 01/01/2025 – 123 Main St";
        var blocks = new List<OcrTextBlock>
        {
            new OcrTextBlock("MOCK EVENT", 0.95f, new BoundingBox(10, 10, 200, 30)),
            new OcrTextBlock("01/01/2025", 0.90f, new BoundingBox(10, 50, 150, 25)),
            new OcrTextBlock("123 Main St", 0.92f, new BoundingBox(10, 80, 180, 25))
        };


        var result = new OcrResult(
            RequestId: payload.RequestId,
            RawText: raw,
            Blocks: blocks,
            Confidence: 0.92f,
            ProcessedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow);


        return Task.FromResult(result);
    }
}


What you get: OCR contracts, a clean IOcrService interface and a ready‑to‑use mock implementation (injectable via DI for early pipelines). Real providers can be swapped in later without touching downstream code.

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/INormalizationEngine.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Pure, side‑effect free service that converts an <see cref="OcrResult"/>
/// into a <see cref="CandidateEvent"/>.  No database, network or caching may be used.
/// </summary>
public interface INormalizationEngine
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Transform the OCR result into a candidate event.  
    /// Must be deterministic – identical input yields identical output.
    /// </summary>
    Task<CandidateEvent> NormalizeAsync(OcrResult ocrResult);
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------

/// <summary>

public interface INormalizationEngine
{
    /// <summary>

    /// </summary>
    Task<CandidateEvent> NormalizeAsync(OcrResult ocrResult);

📁 WeUP.Application/Ingestion/RegexNormalizationEngine.cs (reference implementation)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Ingestion/RegexNormalizationEngine.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Globalization;
using System.Linq;
using System.Text.RegularExpressions;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ocr;
using WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Application.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Simple deterministic implementation based on regular expressions.
/// Designed for the MVP – can be replaced later with a more sophisticated NLP pipeline.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RegexNormalizationEngine : INormalizationEngine
{
    // Very permissive date patterns for demo purposes.
    private static readonly Regex[] DatePatterns = new[]
    {
        new Regex(@"\b\d{1,2}/\d{1,2}/\d{4}\b", RegexOptions.Compiled),          // 01/31/2025
        new Regex(@"\b\w{3,9}\s+\d{1,2},\s*\d{4}\b", RegexOptions.Compiled),   // Jan 31, 2025
        new Regex(@"\b\d{1,2}\s+\w{3,9}\s+\d{4}\b", RegexOptions.Compiled)    // 31 Jan 2025
    };


    // Simple venue detection – looks for common street suffixes.
    private static readonly Regex LocationPattern = new(@"(?i)\b\d+\s+[A-Za-z]+\s+(St|Ave|Blvd|Rd|Road|Ln|Lane|Pl|Place)\b",
                                                    RegexOptions.Compiled);


    public Task<CandidateEvent> NormalizeAsync(OcrResult ocrResult)
    {
        // 1️⃣ Gather raw fields from OCR blocks.
        var rawFields = new Dictionary<string, string>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        foreach (var block in ocrResult.Blocks)
        {
            // Very naive heuristics – treat each block as a candidate field.
            var text = block.Text.Trim();
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text)) continue;


            // Identify possible date, location or title.
            if (IsDate(text) && !rawFields.ContainsKey("Date"))
                rawFields["Date"] = text;
            else if (IsLocation(text) && !rawFields.ContainsKey("Location"))
                rawFields["Location"] = text;
            else if (!rawFields.ContainsKey("Title"))
                rawFields["Title"] = text;
        }


        // 2️⃣ Extract Title (first title‑like block)
        rawFields.TryGetValue("Title", out var title);


        // 3️⃣ Parse Date → InferredStartUtc
        DateTimeOffset? start = null;
        if (rawFields.TryGetValue("Date", out var dateStr))
        {
            start = ParseDate(dateStr);
        }


        // 4️⃣ Location text (raw)
        rawFields.TryGetValue("Location", out var locationText);


        // 5️⃣ Confidence – average confidence of blocks that contributed to any field
        var usedBlocks = ocrResult.Blocks
                                  .Where(b => rawFields.Values.Any(v => b.Text.Contains(v, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                                  .Select(b => b.Confidence)
                                  .ToArray();


        var confidence = usedBlocks.Length > 0 ? usedBlocks.Average() : 0f;


        var candidate = new CandidateEvent(
            CandidateId: Guid.NewGuid(),
            RequestId: ocrResult.RequestId,
            Title: title,
            InferredStartUtc: start,
            RawLocationText: locationText,
            OverallExtractionConfidence: (float)confidence,
            RawFields: rawFields);


        return Task.FromResult(candidate);
    }


    private static bool IsDate(string text) => DatePatterns.Any(p => p.IsMatch(text));


    private static bool IsLocation(string text) => LocationPattern.IsMatch(text);


    private static DateTimeOffset? ParseDate(string text)
    {
        foreach (var pattern in DatePatterns)
        {
            var match = pattern.Match(text);
            if (!match.Success) continue;


            var candidate = match.Value;
            // Try several standard formats.
            var formats = new[]
            {
                "M/d/yyyy", "MM/dd/yyyy", "MMM d, yyyy", "d MMM yyyy", "yyyy-MM-dd"
            };
            if (DateTimeOffset.TryParseExact(candidate,
                                            formats,
                                            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                                            DateTimeStyles.AssumeUniversal,
                                            out var parsed))
            {
                return parsed;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

/// <summary>
/// Simple deterministic implementation based on regular expressions.
/// Designed for the MVP – can be replaced later with a more sophisticated NLP pipeline.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RegexNormalizationEngine : INormalizationEngine
{
    // Very permissive date patterns for demo purposes.
    private static readonly Regex[] DatePatterns = new[]
    {
        new Regex(@"\b\d{1,2}/\d{1,2}/\d{4}\b", RegexOptions.Compiled),          // 01/31/2025
        new Regex(@"\b\w{3,9}\s+\d{1,2},\s*\d{4}\b", RegexOptions.Compiled),   // Jan 31, 2025
        new Regex(@"\b\d{1,2}\s+\w{3,9}\s+\d{4}\b", RegexOptions.Compiled)    // 31 Jan 2025
    };


    // Simple venue detection – looks for common street suffixes.
    private static readonly Regex LocationPattern = new(@"(?i)\b\d+\s+[A-Za-z]+\s+(St|Ave|Blvd|Rd|Road|Ln|Lane|Pl|Place)\b",
                                                    RegexOptions.Compiled);


    public Task<CandidateEvent> NormalizeAsync(OcrResult ocrResult)
    {

        {

                rawFields["Date"] = text;
            else if (IsLocation(text) && !rawFields.ContainsKey("Location"))
                rawFields["Location"] = text;
            else if (!rawFields.ContainsKey("Title"))
                rawFields["Title"] = text;
        }


        // 2️⃣ Extract Title (first title‑like block)
        rawFields.TryGetValue("Title", out var title);


        // 3️⃣ Parse Date → InferredStartUtc
        DateTimeOffset? start = null;

        {
            start = ParseDate(dateStr);
        }


        // 4️⃣ Location text (raw)
        rawFields.TryGetValue("Location", out var locationText);


        // 5️⃣ Confidence – average confidence of blocks that contributed to any field
        var usedBlocks = ocrResult.Blocks
                                  .Where(b => rawFields.Values.Any(v => b.Text.Contains(v, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)))
                                  .Select(b => b.Confidence)
                                  .ToArray();


        var confidence = usedBlocks.Length > 0 ? usedBlocks.Average() : 0f;


        var candidate = new CandidateEvent(
            CandidateId: Guid.NewGuid(),
            RequestId: ocrResult.RequestId,
            Title: title,
            InferredStartUtc: start,
            RawLocationText: locationText,
            OverallExtractionConfidence: (float)confidence,
            RawFields: rawFields);


        return Task.FromResult(candidate);
    }


    private static bool IsDate(string text) => DatePatterns.Any(p => p.IsMatch(text));


    private static bool IsLocation(string text) => LocationPattern.IsMatch(text);


    private static DateTimeOffset? ParseDate(string text)
    {
        foreach (var pattern in DatePatterns)
        {
            var match = pattern.Match(text);
            if (!match.Success) continue;


            var candidate = match.Value;
            // Try several standard formats.
            var formats = new[]
            {
                "M/d/yyyy", "MM/dd/yyyy", "MMM d, yyyy", "d MMM yyyy", "yyyy-MM-dd"
            };
            if (DateTimeOffset.TryParseExact(candidate,
                                            formats,
                                            CultureInfo.InvariantCulture,
                                            DateTimeStyles.AssumeUniversal,
                                            out var parsed))
            {
                return parsed;
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}

csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Ingestion/ConfidenceVector.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Aggregated confidence for the three core dimensions of a candidate event.
/// </summary>
public sealed record ConfidenceVector(
    float TemporalConfidence,   // confidence of inferred start/end time
    float SpatialConfidence,    // confidence of location extraction
    float SemanticConfidence)   // confidence of title / description extraction
{
    /// <summary>
    /// When any dimension falls below the safety threshold (default 0.8) the event
    /// must be reviewed manually.
    /// </summary>
    public bool RequiresManualReview => 
        TemporalConfidence < 0.8f ||
        SpatialConfidence  < 0.8f ||
        SemanticConfidence < 0.8f;
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Ingestion/ConfidenceVector.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;

    float TemporalConfidence,   // confidence of inferred start/end time
    float SpatialConfidence,    // confidence of location extraction
    float SemanticConfidence)   // confidence of title / description extraction
{
    /// <summary>
    /// When any dimension falls below the safety threshold (default 0.8) the event
    /// must be reviewed manually.
    /// </summary>
    public bool RequiresManualReview => 
        TemporalConfidence < 0.8f ||
        SpatialConfidence  < 0.8f ||
        SemanticConfidence < 0.8f;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Ingestion/IEvidenceTracker.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Service responsible for persisting evidence records (append‑only) and
/// calculating the deterministic <see cref="ConfidenceVector"/> for a candidate.
/// </summary>
public interface IEvidenceTracker
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Append a new evidence record to the data store.
    /// Implementations must guarantee idempotency for the same (CandidateId, Source, RawMatchedText) triple.
    /// </summary>
    Task AttachEvidenceAsync(EvidenceRecord record);


    /// <summary>
    /// Compute the confidence vector for the specified candidate by aggregating all
    /// evidence records that belong to it.
    /// </summary>
    Task<ConfidenceVector> CalculateVectorAsync(Guid candidateId);
}

    /// </summary>

📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/EvidenceTracker.cs (EF Core implementation)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/EvidenceTracker.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Options;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// EF Core backed implementation of <see cref="IEvidenceTracker"/>.
/// Stores evidence in the table `IngestionEvidence` (see EF configuration below).
/// </summary>
public sealed class EvidenceTracker : IEvidenceTracker
{
    private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
    private readonly ILogger<EvidenceTracker> _log;
    private readonly ConfidenceOptions _options;


    public EvidenceTracker(WeUpDbContext db,
                           ILogger<EvidenceTracker> log,
                           IOptions<ConfidenceOptions> options)
    {
        _db = db;
        _log = log;
        _options = options.Value;
    }


    public async Task AttachEvidenceAsync(EvidenceRecord record)
    {
        // Simple idempotency: ignore if a record with same CandidateId+Source+RawMatchedText already exists.
        var exists = await _db.IngestionEvidence.AnyAsync(e =>
            e.CandidateId == record.CandidateId &&
            e.Source == record.Source &&
            e.RawMatchedText == record.RawMatchedText);


        if (exists)
        {
            _log.LogDebug("Duplicate evidence ignored for Candidate {CandidateId}, Source {Source}",
                          record.CandidateId, record.Source);
            return;
        }


        var entity = new IngestionEvidenceEntity
        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            CandidateId = record.CandidateId,
            Source = record.Source,
            RawMatchedText = record.RawMatchedText,
            LocalConfidence = record.LocalConfidence,
            CreatedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow
        };


        _db.IngestionEvidence.Add(entity);
        await _db.SaveChangesAsync();
        _log.LogInformation("Evidence attached: Candidate {CandidateId}, Source {Source}",
                           record.CandidateId, record.Source);
    }


    public async Task<ConfidenceVector> CalculateVectorAsync(Guid candidateId)
    {
        var evidences = await _db.IngestionEvidence
                                 .Where(e => e.CandidateId == candidateId)
                                 .ToListAsync();


        if (!evidences.Any())
        {
            // No evidence → all confidences are 0.
            return new ConfidenceVector(0f, 0f, 0f);
        }


        // Map source to dimension – simple heuristic:
        //   * any source containing "date"   => Temporal
        //   * any source containing "loc" or "venue" => Spatial
        //   * everything else               => Semantic
        var temporal = evidences
            .Where(e => e.Source.Contains("date", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Select(e => e.LocalConfidence);
        var spatial = evidences
            .Where(e => e.Source.Contains("loc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                        e.Source.Contains("venue", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Select(e => e.LocalConfidence);
        var semantic = evidences
            .Where(e => !e.Source.Contains("date", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !e.Source.Contains("loc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !e.Source.Contains("venue", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Select(e => e.LocalConfidence);


        float temporalConf = temporal.Any() ? temporal.Max() : 0f;
        float spatialConf  = spatial.Any()  ? spatial.Max()  : 0f;
        float semanticConf = semantic.Any() ? semantic.Max() : 0f;


        return new ConfidenceVector(temporalConf, spatialConf, semanticConf);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/EvidenceTracker.cs

namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// EF Core backed implementation of <see cref="IEvidenceTracker"/>.
/// Stores evidence in the table `IngestionEvidence` (see EF configuration below).
/// </summary>
public sealed class EvidenceTracker : IEvidenceTracker
{
    private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
    private readonly ILogger<EvidenceTracker> _log;
    private readonly ConfidenceOptions _options;


    public EvidenceTracker(WeUpDbContext db,
                           ILogger<EvidenceTracker> log,
                           IOptions<ConfidenceOptions> options)
    {
        _db = db;
        _log = log;
        _options = options.Value;
    }


    public async Task AttachEvidenceAsync(EvidenceRecord record)
    {

            e.CandidateId == record.CandidateId &&
            e.Source == record.Source &&
            e.RawMatchedText == record.RawMatchedText);


        if (exists)

                          record.CandidateId, record.Source);
            return;
        }

        {
            Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
            CandidateId = record.CandidateId,
            Source = record.Source,
            RawMatchedText = record.RawMatchedText,
            LocalConfidence = record.LocalConfidence,
            CreatedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow
        };


        _db.IngestionEvidence.Add(entity);
        await _db.SaveChangesAsync();
        _log.LogInformation("Evidence attached: Candidate {CandidateId}, Source {Source}",
                           record.CandidateId, record.Source);
    }


    public async Task<ConfidenceVector> CalculateVectorAsync(Guid candidateId)
    {
        var evidences = await _db.IngestionEvidence
                                 .Where(e => e.CandidateId == candidateId)
                                 .ToListAsync();

            return new ConfidenceVector(0f, 0f, 0f);

        //   * any source containing "date"   => Temporal
        //   * any source containing "loc" or "venue" => Spatial
        //   * everything else               => Semantic

            .Where(e => e.Source.Contains("date", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Select(e => e.LocalConfidence);
        var spatial = evidences
            .Where(e => e.Source.Contains("loc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ||
                        e.Source.Contains("venue", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Select(e => e.LocalConfidence);
        var semantic = evidences
            .Where(e => !e.Source.Contains("date", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !e.Source.Contains("loc", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) &&
                        !e.Source.Contains("venue", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            .Select(e => e.LocalConfidence);


        float temporalConf = temporal.Any() ? temporal.Max() : 0f;
        float spatialConf  = spatial.Any()  ? spatial.Max()  : 0f;
        float semanticConf = semantic.Any() ? semantic.Max() : 0f;


        return new ConfidenceVector(temporalConf, spatialConf, semanticConf);
    }
}


📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Entities/IngestionEvidenceEntity.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Entities/IngestionEvidenceEntity.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


/// <summary>
/// EF Core entity representing a single evidence record.  
/// Table: IngestionEvidence (append‑only).
/// </summary>
public sealed class IngestionEvidenceEntity
{
    public Guid Id { get; set; }
    public Guid CandidateId { get; set; }
    public string Source { get; set; } = default!;
    public string RawMatchedText { get; set; } = default!;
    public float LocalConfidence { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc { get; set; }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Entities/IngestionEvidenceEntity.cs

using System;

using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Configurations;


public sealed class IngestionEvidenceConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<IngestionEvidenceEntity>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<IngestionEvidenceEntity> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("IngestionEvidence");


        builder.HasKey(e => e.Id);
        builder.Property(e => e.Source).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(e => e.RawMatchedText).IsRequired();


        // Append‑only semantics – prevent accidental updates/deletes via EF.
        builder.Metadata.SetAfterSaveBehavior(PropertySaveBehavior.Throw);
    }
}

5️⃣ M1‑P05 – Create Ingestion Job Lifecycle and State Tracking v1.0
📁 WeUP.Contracts/Ingestion/IngestionJob.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Ingestion/IngestionJob.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Possible lifecycle states for an ingestion job.
/// Transitions must be monotonic (see IngestionJobManager implementation for rules).
/// </summary>
public enum IngestionJobStatus
{
    Received,           // request stored, awaiting processing
    OcrProcessing,     // OCR service is running
    Normalizing,       // Normalization engine is running
    PendingReview,     // Candidate ready for moderation queue
    Rejected,          // Moderation rejected / validation failed
    Published,         // Candidate approved and persisted as EventAggregate
    Failed             // Any unhandled exception or unrecoverable error
}


/// <summary>
/// Persistence model for a single ingestion job.
/// </summary>
public sealed record IngestionJob(
    Guid JobId,
    Guid RequestId,
    IngestionJobStatus Status,
    DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc,
    DateTimeOffset UpdatedAtUtc,
    string? FailureReason);


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Ingestion/IngestionJob.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Possible lifecycle states for an ingestion job.
/// Transitions must be monotonic (see IngestionJobManager implementation for rules).
/// </summary>
public enum IngestionJobStatus
{
    Received,           // request stored, awaiting processing
    OcrProcessing,     // OCR service is running
    Normalizing,       // Normalization engine is running
    PendingReview,     // Candidate ready for moderation queue
    Rejected,          // Moderation rejected / validation failed
    Published,         // Candidate approved and persisted as EventAggregate
    Failed             // Any unhandled exception or unrecoverable error
}


/// <summary>
/// Persistence model for a single ingestion job.
/// </summary>
public sealed record IngestionJob(
    Guid JobId,
    Guid RequestId,
    IngestionJobStatus Status,
    DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc,
    DateTimeOffset UpdatedAtUtc,
    string? FailureReason);


📁 WeUP.Application/Ingestion/IIngestionJobManager.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Ingestion/IIngestionJobManager.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Application.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Service responsible for creating and managing ingestion job state.
/// </summary>
public interface IIngestionJobManager
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Creates a new job record in the “Received” state.
    /// </summary>
    Task<IngestionJob> CreateJobAsync(IngestionRequest request);


    /// <summary>
    /// Transition a job to <paramref name="newStatus"/>.
    /// Throws <see cref="InvalidOperationException"/> if the transition is not monotonic.
    /// Optional <paramref name="failureReason"/> is stored when moving to the <c>Failed</c> state.
    /// </summary>
    Task TransitionJobAsync(Guid jobId, IngestionJobStatus newStatus, string? failureReason = null);
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Ingestion/IIngestionJobManager.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Application.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// Service responsible for creating and managing ingestion job state.
/// </summary>
public interface IIngestionJobManager
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Creates a new job record in the “Received” state.
    /// </summary>
    Task<IngestionJob> CreateJobAsync(IngestionRequest request);


    /// <summary>
    /// Transition a job to <paramref name="newStatus"/>.
    /// Throws <see cref="InvalidOperationException"/> if the transition is not monotonic.
    /// Optional <paramref name="failureReason"/> is stored when moving to the <c>Failed</c> state.
    /// </summary>
    Task TransitionJobAsync(Guid jobId, IngestionJobStatus newStatus, string? failureReason = null);
}


📁 WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/IngestionJobManager.cs
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Ingestion/IngestionJobManager.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Application.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// EF Core backed implementation that enforces monotonic state transitions
/// and logs every change.
/// </summary>
public sealed class IngestionJobManager : IIngestionJobManager
{
    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<IngestionJobStatus, IngestionJobStatus[]> AllowedTransitions
        = new Dictionary<IngestionJobStatus, IngestionJobStatus[]>
    {
        [IngestionJobStatus.Received]      = new[] { IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing]= new[] { IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing]  = new[] { IngestionJobStatus.PendingReview, IngestionJobStatus.Published, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.PendingReview]= new[] { IngestionJobStatus.Published, IngestionJobStatus.Rejected, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.Rejected]     = Array.Empty<IngestionJobStatus>(),
        [IngestionJobStatus.Published]    = Array.Empty<IngestionJobStatus>(),
        [IngestionJobStatus.Failed]       = Array.Empty<IngestionJobStatus>()
    };


    private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
    private readonly ILogger<IngestionJobManager> _log;


    public IngestionJobManager(WeUpDbContext db, ILogger<IngestionJobManager> log)
    {
        _db = db;
        _log = log;
    }


    public async Task<IngestionJob> CreateJobAsync(IngestionRequest request)
    {
        var job = new IngestionJob(
            JobId: Guid.NewGuid(),
            RequestId: request.RequestId,
            Status: IngestionJobStatus.Received,
            CreatedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            UpdatedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            FailureReason: null);


        var entity = new IngestionJobEntity
        {
            JobId = job.JobId,
            RequestId = job.RequestId,
            Status = job.Status,
            CreatedAtUtc = job.CreatedAtUtc,
            UpdatedAtUtc = job.UpdatedAtUtc,
            FailureReason = null
        };


        _db.IngestionJobs.Add(entity);
        await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


        _log.LogInformation("Ingestion job {JobId} created (Received).", job.JobId);
        return job;
    }


    public async Task TransitionJobAsync(Guid jobId, IngestionJobStatus newStatus, string? failureReason = null)
    {
        var entity = await _db.IngestionJobs
                             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(j => j.JobId == jobId);


        if (entity == null)
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Job {jobId} not found.");


        var current = entity.Status;


        // Validate monotonic transition
        if (!AllowedTransitions[current].Contains(newStatus))
        {
            _log.LogError("Invalid state transition {JobId}: {From} => {To}",
                          jobId, current, newStatus);
            throw new InvalidOperationException($"Invalid transition from {current} to {newStatus}");
        }


        entity.Status = newStatus;
        entity.UpdatedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
        entity.FailureReason = failureReason;


        // Concurrency protection – row version/timestamp column (see EF config)
        _db.IngestionJobs.Update(entity);
        await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


        _log.LogInformation("Ingestion job {JobId} transitioned {From} => {To}",
                            jobId, current, newStatus);
    }
}

namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion;


/// <summary>
/// EF Core backed implementation that enforces monotonic state transitions
/// and logs every change.
/// </summary>
public sealed class IngestionJobManager : IIngestionJobManager
{
    private static readonly IReadOnlyDictionary<IngestionJobStatus, IngestionJobStatus[]> AllowedTransitions
        = new Dictionary<IngestionJobStatus, IngestionJobStatus[]>
    {
        [IngestionJobStatus.Received]      = new[] { IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing]= new[] { IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing]  = new[] { IngestionJobStatus.PendingReview, IngestionJobStatus.Published, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.PendingReview]= new[] { IngestionJobStatus.Published, IngestionJobStatus.Rejected, IngestionJobStatus.Failed },
        [IngestionJobStatus.Rejected]     = Array.Empty<IngestionJobStatus>(),
        [IngestionJobStatus.Published]    = Array.Empty<IngestionJobStatus>(),
        [IngestionJobStatus.Failed]       = Array.Empty<IngestionJobStatus>()
    };


    private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
    private readonly ILogger<IngestionJobManager> _log;

    }


    public async Task TransitionJobAsync(Guid jobId, IngestionJobStatus newStatus, string? failureReason = null)
    {

                             .FirstOrDefaultAsync(j => j.JobId == jobId);

        }

        entity.UpdatedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;

        await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


        _log.LogInformation("Ingestion job {JobId} transitioned {From} => {To}",
                            jobId, current, newStatus);
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Entities/IngestionJobEntity.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations;
using System.ComponentModel.DataAnnotations.Schema;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


/// <summary>
/// EF Core representation of an ingestion job. Includes a RowVersion for optimistic concurrency.
/// </summary>
public sealed class IngestionJobEntity
{
    [Key]
    public Guid JobId { get; set; }


    public Guid RequestId { get; set; }


    public IngestionJobStatus Status { get; set; }


    public DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc { get; set; }


    public DateTimeOffset UpdatedAtUtc { get; set; }


    public string? FailureReason { get; set; }


    // Optimistic concurrency token
    [Timestamp]
    public byte[] RowVersion { get; set; } = null!;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/IngestionJobConfiguration.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Configurations;


public sealed class IngestionJobConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<IngestionJobEntity>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<IngestionJobEntity> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("IngestionJobs");


        builder.HasKey(e => e.JobId);
        builder.Property(e => e.Status)
               .IsRequired()
               .HasConversion<string>(); // store enum as string for readability


        builder.Property(e => e.RowVersion)
               .IsRowVersion(); // ensures concurrency exception on race conditions
    }
}

📁 WeUP.Tests/Ingestion/IngestionJobManagerTests.cs (xUnit skeleton)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Tests/Ingestion/IngestionJobManagerTests.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging.Abstractions;
using WeUP.Application.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using Xunit;


namespace WeUP.Tests.Ingestion;


public sealed class IngestionJobManagerTests
{
    private WeUpDbContext BuildContext()
    {
        var options = new DbContextOptionsBuilder<WeUpDbContext>()
            .UseInMemoryDatabase(Guid.NewGuid().ToString())
            .Options;


        return new WeUpDbContext(options);
    }


    [Fact]
    public async Task CreateJob_InitialState_IsReceived()
    {
        // Arrange
        var ctx = BuildContext();
        var manager = new IngestionJobManager(ctx, NullLogger<IngestionJobManager>.Instance);
        var request = new IngestionRequest(
            RequestId: Guid.NewGuid(),
            SourceType: IngestionSourceType.ImageUpload,
            RawInput: "base64data",
            SubmittedByUserId: null,
            SubmittedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            Metadata: null);


        // Act
        var job = await manager.CreateJobAsync(request);


        // Assert
        Assert.Equal(IngestionJobStatus.Received, job.Status);
        Assert.NotNull(await ctx.IngestionJobs.FirstOrDefaultAsync(j => j.JobId == job.JobId));
    }


    [Fact]
    public async Task Transition_ValidMonotonicPath_Succeeds()
    {
        var ctx = BuildContext();
        var manager = new IngestionJobManager(ctx, NullLogger<IngestionJobManager>.Instance);


        var request = new IngestionRequest(
            RequestId: Guid.NewGuid(),
            SourceType: IngestionSourceType.ImageUpload,
            RawInput: "base64data",
            SubmittedByUserId: null,
            SubmittedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            Metadata: null);
        var job = await manager.CreateJobAsync(request);


        // Valid chain: Received → OcrProcessing → Normalizing → PendingReview → Published
        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing);
        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing);
        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.PendingReview);
        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.Published);
    }


    [Fact]
    public async Task Transition_InvalidBacktrack_Throws()
    {
        var ctx = BuildContext();
        var manager = new IngestionJobManager(ctx, NullLogger<IngestionJobManager>.Instance);


        var request = new IngestionRequest(
            RequestId: Guid.NewGuid(),
            SourceType: IngestionSourceType.ImageUpload,
            RawInput: "base64data",
            SubmittedByUserId: null,
            SubmittedAtUtc: DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
            Metadata: null);
        var job = await manager.CreateJobAsync(request);


        // Move to Normalizing
        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing);
        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing);


        // Attempt illegal backtrack to OcrProcessing → should throw
        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(async () =>
            await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing));
    }
}

        await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.Normalizing);

        await Assert.ThrowsAsync<InvalidOperationException>(async () =>
            await manager.TransitionJobAsync(job.JobId, IngestionJobStatus.OcrProcessing));

* Run the unit‑test project (dotnet test) – all tests should pass.
* The ingestion pipeline is now fully wired: Adapter → OCR → Normalization → Evidence → Job tracking – ready for the next bundles (deduplication, moderation, map, etc.).
Below are five complete, copy‑paste‑ready prompt blocks that implement Bundle M2 – Deterministic Deduplication & Merge v1.0.
Each block contains:
1. Context & Objective – short narrative.
2. Contracts – C# 11 records / enums / interfaces (placed in the indicated folder).
3. Reference Implementation (skeleton) – deterministic, side‑effect‑free code that you can drop into the project.
4. Rules & Constraints – what the implementation must obey.
5. Acceptance Criteria – checklist for reviewers / CI.
Folder conventions (create these if they do not exist):
* WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe – all contract files.
* WeUP.Domain/Dedupe – domain‑level interfaces.
* WeUP.Application/Dedupe – concrete services / implementations.
* WeUP.Infrastructure/Dedupe – EF Core entities & configurations for provenance/merge‑history.
* WeUP.Tests/Dedupe – unit‑test skeletons (xUnit).
________________


M2‑P06 — Define Canonical Deduplication Strategy and Matching Heuristics v1.0
markdown
# Context
We need a **single, deterministic deduplication strategy** that can compare an incoming `CandidateEvent` against existing canonical `EventAggregate`s and decide whether the two represent the same real‑world event.


# Objective
Create the contracts that describe:
* The **assessment result** (`DuplicateAssessment`) and its **severity level** (`DuplicateAssessmentLevel`).  
* The **weighted matching dimensions** (`MatchDimension` and `MatchScoreBreakdown`).  
* A **service contract** (`IDeduplicationService`) that returns an assessment based on those dimensions.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe/DeduplicationContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// High‑level classification of how two events relate to each other.
/// </summary>
public enum DuplicateAssessmentLevel
{
    ExactDuplicate,      // All key fields match 100 %
    ProbableDuplicate,   // Very high confidence (≥ 0.9) but minor differences
    PossibleDuplicate,   // Moderate confidence (0.7 – 0.9)
    Distinct,            // Confidently different events
    Conflict             // Critical mismatches that require manual review
}


/// <summary>
/// Individual dimension used for scoring.
/// </summary>
public enum MatchDimension
{
    Title,
    Venue,
    StartTime,
    Location,   // Geo‑coordinates or address string
    SourceHash  // SHA‑256 of the original flyer image
}


/// <summary>
/// Breakdown of the similarity score per dimension.
/// </summary>
public sealed record MatchScoreBreakdown(
    MatchDimension Dimension,
    float Score);          // 0.0 – 1.0 (higher = more similar)


/// <summary>
/// Full deduplication assessment for a candidate vs. a canonical event.
/// </summary>
public sealed record DuplicateAssessment(
    Guid CandidateId,
    Guid CanonicalEventId,
    DuplicateAssessmentLevel Level,
    MatchScoreBreakdown[] Scores,
    string? Explanation);     // Human‑readable reasoning (optional)


# Context
We need a **single, deterministic deduplication strategy** that can compare an incoming `CandidateEvent` against existing canonical `EventAggregate`s and decide whether the two represent the same real‑world event.


# Objective
Create the contracts that describe:
* The **assessment result** (`DuplicateAssessment`) and its **severity level** (`DuplicateAssessmentLevel`).  
* The **weighted matching dimensions** (`MatchDimension` and `MatchScoreBreakdown`).  
* A **service contract** (`IDeduplicationService`) that returns an assessment based on those dimensions.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)

Interface (place in WeUP.Domain/Dedupe)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Dedupe/IDeduplicationService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic service that evaluates whether a candidate event duplicates an existing
/// canonical event.  Implementations must be pure (no DB writes) – only reads are allowed.
/// </summary>
public interface IDeduplicationService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Compare <paramref name="candidate"/> against the supplied list of <paramref name="canonicalEvents"/>
    /// and return the *best* assessment (the one with the highest confidence).
    /// If the list is empty, return a Distinct assessment.
    /// </summary>
    Task<DuplicateAssessment> AssessAsync(
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        IReadOnlyCollection<EventAggregate> canonicalEvents);
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Dedupe/IDeduplicationService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
