namespace WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic service that evaluates whether a candidate event duplicates an existing
/// canonical event.  Implementations must be pure (no DB writes) – only reads are allowed.
/// </summary>
public interface IDeduplicationService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Compare <paramref name="candidate"/> against the supplied list of <paramref name="canonicalEvents"/>
    /// and return the *best* assessment (the one with the highest confidence).
    /// If the list is empty, return a Distinct assessment.
    /// </summary>
    Task<DuplicateAssessment> AssessAsync(
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        IReadOnlyCollection<EventAggregate> canonicalEvents);
}


Reference implementation (place in WeUP.Application/Dedupe/WeightedDeduplicationService.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/WeightedDeduplicationService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic, weight‑based deduplication service.
/// Scoring is performed entirely in‑memory; no side effects.
/// </summary>
public sealed class WeightedDeduplicationService : IDeduplicationService
{
    private readonly ILogger<WeightedDeduplicationService> _log;
    // Weight configuration – can be moved to options if needed.
    private const float TitleWeight      = 0.35f;
    private const float VenueWeight      = 0.20f;
    private const float TimeWeight       = 0.25f;
    private const float LocationWeight   = 0.15f;
    private const float HashWeight       = 0.05f;


    public WeightedDeduplicationService(ILogger<WeightedDeduplicationService> log)
    {
        _log = log;
    }


    public Task<DuplicateAssessment> AssessAsync(
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        IReadOnlyCollection<EventAggregate> canonicalEvents)
    {
        // If there are no canonical events yet, the candidate is definitely distinct.
        if (!canonicalEvents.Any())
        {
            var distinct = new DuplicateAssessment(
                candidate.CandidateId,
                Guid.Empty,
                DuplicateAssessmentLevel.Distinct,
                Array.Empty<MatchScoreBreakdown>(),
                "No existing events to compare against.");


            return Task.FromResult(distinct);
        }


        // Compute a score for each canonical event.
        var best = canonicalEvents
            .Select(evt => EvaluatePair(candidate, evt))
            .OrderByDescending(a => OverallScore(a.Scores))
            .First();


        _log.LogInformation(
            "Deduplication assessment for Candidate {CandidateId} → Event {EventId} = {Level}",
            candidate.CandidateId,
            best.CanonicalEventId,
            best.Level);


        return Task.FromResult(best);
    }


    // ---------- private helpers ----------
    private static DuplicateAssessment EvaluatePair(CandidateEvent candidate, EventAggregate evt)
    {
        var scores = new List<MatchScoreBreakdown>();


        // ---- Title similarity (simple case‑insensitive equality + Levenshtein fallback) ----
        var titleScore = StringSimilarity(candidate.Title, evt.Title);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.Title, titleScore));


        // ---- Venue similarity ----
        var venueScore = StringSimilarity(candidate.RawLocationText, evt.VenueName);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.Venue, venueScore));


        // ---- Start‑time similarity (difference in minutes) ----
        var timeScore = TimeSimilarity(candidate.InferredStartUtc, evt.StartUtc);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.StartTime, timeScore));


        // ---- Geo‑location similarity (Haversine distance) ----
        var locationScore = GeoSimilarity(candidate.RawLocationText, evt.Location);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.Location, locationScore));


        // ---- Source hash match (exact equality) ----
        var hashScore = candidate.RawFields.TryGetValue("ContentHash", out var candHash) &&
                        evt.SourceHash.Equals(candHash, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        ? 1f : 0f;
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.SourceHash, hashScore));


        // Combine weighted scores into a final confidence.
        var weighted = (titleScore    * TitleWeight) +
                       (venueScore    * VenueWeight) +
                       (timeScore     * TimeWeight) +
                       (locationScore * LocationWeight) +
                       (hashScore     * HashWeight);


        var level = Classify(weighted, scores);


        var explanation = $"WeightedScore={weighted:F3}; Details=[{string.Join("; ", scores.Select(s => $"{s.Dimension}:{s.Score:F2"))}]";


        return new DuplicateAssessment(
            candidate.CandidateId,
            evt.EventId,
            level,
            scores.ToArray(),
            explanation);
    }


    private static DuplicateAssessmentLevel Classify(float weightedScore, List<MatchScoreBreakdown> scores)
    {
        // Simple deterministic thresholds.
        if (weightedScore >= 0.95f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ExactDuplicate;
        if (weightedScore >= 0.90f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ProbableDuplicate;
        if (weightedScore >= 0.80f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.PossibleDuplicate;
        if (weightedScore >= 0.60f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.Conflict;
        return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.Distinct;
    }


    private static float OverallScore(MatchScoreBreakdown[] scores)
        => scores.Sum(s => s.Score) / scores.Length;


    // ---------- similarity helpers ----------
    private static float StringSimilarity(string? a, string? b)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b))
            return 0f;


        // Case‑insensitive equality gets a perfect score.
        if (a.Equals(b, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return 1f;


        // Otherwise, use a very cheap Levenshtein‑based similarity (0‑1).
        int distance = LevenshteinDistance(a, b);
        int maxLen = Math.Max(a.Length, b.Length);
        return 1f - ((float)distance / maxLen);
    }


    private static int LevenshteinDistance(string a, string b)
    {
        var costs = new int[b.Length + 1];


        for (int j = 0; j <= b.Length; j++) costs[j] = j;


        for (int i = 1; i <= a.Length; i++)
        {
            costs[0] = i;
            int corner = i - 1;


            for (int j = 1; j <= b.Length; j++)
            {
                int upper = costs[j];
                int cost = a[i - 1] == b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
                costs[j] = Math.Min(Math.Min(costs[j - 1] + 1, costs[j] + 1), corner + cost);
                corner = upper;
            }
        }


        return costs[b.Length];
    }


    private static float TimeSimilarity(DateTimeOffset? a, DateTimeOffset? b)
    {
        if (!a.HasValue || !b.HasValue) return 0f;
        var diff = Math.Abs((a.Value - b.Value).TotalMinutes);
        // Within 15 min ⇒ perfect, 0‑30 min ⇒ 0.8, > 2 h ⇒ 0
        if (diff <= 15) return 1f;
        if (diff <= 30) return 0.8f;
        if (diff <= 120) return 0.4f;
        return 0f;
    }


    private static float GeoSimilarity(string? locationText, GeoPoint? geo)
    {
        // For MVP we treat the location text as a fallback string similarity.
        // If a real GeoPoint is available, compute haversine distance.
        if (geo.HasValue && TryParseLocation(locationText, out var candidateGeo))
        {
            var meters = HaversineMeters(geo.Value, candidateGeo);
            if (meters < 50) return 1f;        // < 50 m
            if (meters < 200) return 0.8f;    // 50‑200 m
            if (meters < 1000) return 0.5f;   // 200‑1000 m
            return 0f;
        }


        // Fallback to string similarity.
        return StringSimilarity(locationText, geo?.ToString());
    }


    private static bool TryParseLocation(string? txt, out GeoPoint point)
    {
        // Very naive “lat,lon” parser – real implementation would use a geocoder.
        point = default;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txt)) return false;
        var parts = txt.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
        if (parts.Length != 2) return false;
        if (double.TryParse(parts[0], out var lat) && double.TryParse(parts[1], out var lon))
        {
            point = new GeoPoint(lat, lon);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }


    private static double HaversineMeters(GeoPoint a, GeoPoint b)
    {
        const double R = 6371000; // Earth radius in metres
        double dLat = ToRad(b.Latitude - a.Latitude);
        double dLon = ToRad(b.Longitude - a.Longitude);
        double lat1 = ToRad(a.Latitude);
        double lat2 = ToRad(b.Latitude);


        double h = Math.Sin(dLat / 2) * Math.Sin(dLat / 2) +
                   Math.Sin(dLon / 2) * Math.Sin(dLon / 2) * Math.Cos(lat1) * Math.Cos(lat2);
        double c = 2 * Math.Atan2(Math.Sqrt(h), Math.Sqrt(1 - h));
        return R * c;
    }


    private static double ToRad(double deg) => deg * Math.PI / 180.0;
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/WeightedDeduplicationService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic, weight‑based deduplication service.
/// Scoring is performed entirely in‑memory; no side effects.
/// </summary>
public sealed class WeightedDeduplicationService : IDeduplicationService
{
    private readonly ILogger<WeightedDeduplicationService> _log;
    // Weight configuration – can be moved to options if needed.
    private const float TitleWeight      = 0.35f;
    private const float VenueWeight      = 0.20f;
    private const float TimeWeight       = 0.25f;
    private const float LocationWeight   = 0.15f;
    private const float HashWeight       = 0.05f;


    public WeightedDeduplicationService(ILogger<WeightedDeduplicationService> log)
    {
        _log = log;
    }


    public Task<DuplicateAssessment> AssessAsync(
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        IReadOnlyCollection<EventAggregate> canonicalEvents)
    {
        // If there are no canonical events yet, the candidate is definitely distinct.
        if (!canonicalEvents.Any())
        {
            var distinct = new DuplicateAssessment(
                candidate.CandidateId,
                Guid.Empty,
                DuplicateAssessmentLevel.Distinct,
                Array.Empty<MatchScoreBreakdown>(),
                "No existing events to compare against.");


            return Task.FromResult(distinct);
        }


        // Compute a score for each canonical event.
        var best = canonicalEvents
            .Select(evt => EvaluatePair(candidate, evt))
            .OrderByDescending(a => OverallScore(a.Scores))
            .First();


        _log.LogInformation(
            "Deduplication assessment for Candidate {CandidateId} → Event {EventId} = {Level}",
            candidate.CandidateId,
            best.CanonicalEventId,
            best.Level);


        return Task.FromResult(best);
    }


    // ---------- private helpers ----------
    private static DuplicateAssessment EvaluatePair(CandidateEvent candidate, EventAggregate evt)
    {
        var scores = new List<MatchScoreBreakdown>();


        // ---- Title similarity (simple case‑insensitive equality + Levenshtein fallback) ----
        var titleScore = StringSimilarity(candidate.Title, evt.Title);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.Title, titleScore));


        // ---- Venue similarity ----
        var venueScore = StringSimilarity(candidate.RawLocationText, evt.VenueName);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.Venue, venueScore));


        // ---- Start‑time similarity (difference in minutes) ----
        var timeScore = TimeSimilarity(candidate.InferredStartUtc, evt.StartUtc);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.StartTime, timeScore));


        // ---- Geo‑location similarity (Haversine distance) ----
        var locationScore = GeoSimilarity(candidate.RawLocationText, evt.Location);
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.Location, locationScore));


        // ---- Source hash match (exact equality) ----
        var hashScore = candidate.RawFields.TryGetValue("ContentHash", out var candHash) &&
                        evt.SourceHash.Equals(candHash, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase)
                        ? 1f : 0f;
        scores.Add(new MatchScoreBreakdown(MatchDimension.SourceHash, hashScore));


        // Combine weighted scores into a final confidence.
        var weighted = (titleScore    * TitleWeight) +
                       (venueScore    * VenueWeight) +
                       (timeScore     * TimeWeight) +
                       (locationScore * LocationWeight) +
                       (hashScore     * HashWeight);


        var level = Classify(weighted, scores);


        var explanation = $"WeightedScore={weighted:F3}; Details=[{string.Join("; ", scores.Select(s => $"{s.Dimension}:{s.Score:F2"))}]";


        return new DuplicateAssessment(
            candidate.CandidateId,
            evt.EventId,
            level,
            scores.ToArray(),
            explanation);
    }


    private static DuplicateAssessmentLevel Classify(float weightedScore, List<MatchScoreBreakdown> scores)
    {
        // Simple deterministic thresholds.
        if (weightedScore >= 0.95f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ExactDuplicate;
        if (weightedScore >= 0.90f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ProbableDuplicate;
        if (weightedScore >= 0.80f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.PossibleDuplicate;
        if (weightedScore >= 0.60f) return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.Conflict;
        return DuplicateAssessmentLevel.Distinct;
    }


    private static float OverallScore(MatchScoreBreakdown[] scores)
        => scores.Sum(s => s.Score) / scores.Length;


    // ---------- similarity helpers ----------
    private static float StringSimilarity(string? a, string? b)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b))
            return 0f;


        // Case‑insensitive equality gets a perfect score.
        if (a.Equals(b, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return 1f;


        // Otherwise, use a very cheap Levenshtein‑based similarity (0‑1).
        int distance = LevenshteinDistance(a, b);
        int maxLen = Math.Max(a.Length, b.Length);
        return 1f - ((float)distance / maxLen);
    }


    private static int LevenshteinDistance(string a, string b)
    {
        var costs = new int[b.Length + 1];


        for (int j = 0; j <= b.Length; j++) costs[j] = j;


        for (int i = 1; i <= a.Length; i++)
        {
            costs[0] = i;
            int corner = i - 1;


            for (int j = 1; j <= b.Length; j++)
            {
                int upper = costs[j];
                int cost = a[i - 1] == b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
                costs[j] = Math.Min(Math.Min(costs[j - 1] + 1, costs[j] + 1), corner + cost);
                corner = upper;
            }
        }


        return costs[b.Length];
    }


    private static float TimeSimilarity(DateTimeOffset? a, DateTimeOffset? b)
    {
        if (!a.HasValue || !b.HasValue) return 0f;
        var diff = Math.Abs((a.Value - b.Value).TotalMinutes);
        // Within 15 min ⇒ perfect, 0‑30 min ⇒ 0.8, > 2 h ⇒ 0
        if (diff <= 15) return 1f;
        if (diff <= 30) return 0.8f;
        if (diff <= 120) return 0.4f;
        return 0f;
    }


    private static float GeoSimilarity(string? locationText, GeoPoint? geo)
    {
        // For MVP we treat the location text as a fallback string similarity.
        // If a real GeoPoint is available, compute haversine distance.
        if (geo.HasValue && TryParseLocation(locationText, out var candidateGeo))
        {
            var meters = HaversineMeters(geo.Value, candidateGeo);
            if (meters < 50) return 1f;        // < 50 m
            if (meters < 200) return 0.8f;    // 50‑200 m
            if (meters < 1000) return 0.5f;   // 200‑1000 m
            return 0f;
        }


        // Fallback to string similarity.
        return StringSimilarity(locationText, geo?.ToString());
    }


    private static bool TryParseLocation(string? txt, out GeoPoint point)
    {
        // Very naive “lat,lon” parser – real implementation would use a geocoder.
        point = default;
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(txt)) return false;
        var parts = txt.Split(',', StringSplitOptions.TrimEntries);
        if (parts.Length != 2) return false;
        if (double.TryParse(parts[0], out var lat) && double.TryParse(parts[1], out var lon))
        {
            point = new GeoPoint(lat, lon);
            return true;
        }
        return false;
    }


    private static double HaversineMeters(GeoPoint a, GeoPoint b)
    {
        const double R = 6371000; // Earth radius in metres
        double dLat = ToRad(b.Latitude - a.Latitude);
        double dLon = ToRad(b.Longitude - a.Longitude);
        double lat1 = ToRad(a.Latitude);
        double lat2 = ToRad(b.Latitude);


        double h = Math.Sin(dLat / 2) * Math.Sin(dLat / 2) +
                   Math.Sin(dLon / 2) * Math.Sin(dLon / 2) * Math.Cos(lat1) * Math.Cos(lat2);
        double c = 2 * Math.Atan2(Math.Sqrt(h), Math.Sqrt(1 - h));
        return R * c;
    }


    private static double ToRad(double deg) => deg * Math.PI / 180.0;
}


Rules & Constraints
* Deterministic – given the same candidate and same canonical event the method always returns the same DuplicateAssessment.
* No writes to the database; the service may only read EventAggregate data.
* Scoring must be pure functions (no randomness, no external calls).
* All weights are hard‑coded (or injected via options) and documented.
Acceptance Criteria
1. DuplicateAssessmentLevel, MatchDimension, MatchScoreBreakdown, DuplicateAssessment compile as immutable records/enums.
2. IDeduplicationService exists with the AssessAsync signature.
3. WeightedDeduplicationService implements the interface, uses only the helper functions shown, and logs the outcome.
4. Unit‑test placeholders (see M2‑P06 test file) demonstrate:
   * Exact duplicate → ExactDuplicate.
   * Slight title change → ProbableDuplicate or PossibleDuplicate.
   * Completely unrelated event → Distinct.
5. The service is registered in DI (e.g., services.AddTransient<IDeduplicationService, WeightedDeduplicationService>();).
________________


M2‑P07 — Implement Fuzzy Matching Across Title, Time, and Venue v1.0
markdown
# Context
The deduplication service (M2‑P06) needs **deterministic fuzzy‑matching helpers** that compute a similarity score (0 – 1) for each domain dimension.


# Objective
Define three matcher interfaces (title, venue, temporal) and concrete deterministic implementations that:
* Normalise whitespace, case and common abbreviations.  
* Use a simple **Levenshtein distance** for strings.  
* Use a **time‑difference bucket** for dates/times.  
* Expose a single method `ComputeSimilarityAsync` returning a float (0 – 1).


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe/FuzzyMatchContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
namespace WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Generic fuzzy matcher for two strings.
/// </summary>
public interface IFuzzyStringMatcher
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Returns a similarity value between 0.0 (completely different) and 1.0 (identical).
    /// </summary>
    Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(string? a, string? b);
}


/// <summary>
/// Matcher for event start times.
/// </summary>
public interface ITemporalMatcher
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Returns a similarity value based on the absolute difference between the two timestamps.
    /// </summary>
    Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(DateTimeOffset? a, DateTimeOffset? b);
}


# Context
The deduplication service (M2‑P06) needs **deterministic fuzzy‑matching helpers** that compute a similarity score (0 – 1) for each domain dimension.


# Objective
Define three matcher interfaces (title, venue, temporal) and concrete deterministic implementations that:
* Normalise whitespace, case and common abbreviations.  
* Use a simple **Levenshtein distance** for strings.  
* Use a **time‑difference bucket** for dates/times.  
* Expose a single method `ComputeSimilarityAsync` returning a float (0 – 1).


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)


```csharp

/// <summary>
/// Generic fuzzy matcher for two strings.
/// </summary>

{
    /// <summary>
    /// Returns a similarity value between 0.0 (completely different) and 1.0 (identical).
    /// </summary>
    Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(string? a, string? b);
}


/// <summary>
/// Matcher for event start times.
/// </summary>
public interface ITemporalMatcher
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Returns a similarity value based on the absolute difference between the two timestamps.
    /// </summary>
    Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(DateTimeOffset? a, DateTimeOffset? b);
}


Implementations (place in WeUP.Application/Dedupe)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/LevenshteinStringMatcher.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic Levenshtein‑based string similarity.
/// Normalises whitespace, case and a small set of common abbreviations.
/// </summary>
public sealed class LevenshteinStringMatcher : IFuzzyStringMatcher
{
    public Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(string? a, string? b)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b))
            return Task.FromResult(0f);


        var normA = Normalise(a);
        var normB = Normalise(b);


        if (normA.Equals(normB, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return Task.FromResult(1f);


        int distance = LevenshteinDistance(normA, normB);
        int max = Math.Max(normA.Length, normB.Length);
        float similarity = 1f - ((float)distance / max);
        return Task.FromResult(similarity);
    }


    private static string Normalise(string input)
    {
        // Trim, collapse whitespace, lower‑case and expand a few abbreviations.
        var lowered = input.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var collapsed = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(lowered, @"\s+", " ");


        // Simple abbreviation map (extend as needed)
        var map = new (string Abbr, string Full)[]
        {
            ("st", "street"),
            ("ave", "avenue"),
            ("blvd", "boulevard"),
            ("rd", "road")
        };


        foreach (var (abbr, full) in map)
        {
            collapsed = collapsed.Replace(abbr, full);
        }


        return collapsed;
    }


    private static int LevenshteinDistance(string a, string b)
    {
        var costs = new int[b.Length + 1];


        for (int j = 0; j <= b.Length; j++) costs[j] = j;


        for (int i = 1; i <= a.Length; i++)
        {
            costs[0] = i;
            int corner = i - 1;


            for (int j = 1; j <= b.Length; j++)
            {
                int upper = costs[j];
                int cost = a[i - 1] == b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
                costs[j] = Math.Min(Math.Min(costs[j - 1] + 1, costs[j] + 1), corner + cost);
                corner = upper;
            }
        }


        return costs[b.Length];
    }
}

using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic Levenshtein‑based string similarity.
/// Normalises whitespace, case and a small set of common abbreviations.
/// </summary>
public sealed class LevenshteinStringMatcher : IFuzzyStringMatcher
{
    public Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(string? a, string? b)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(a) || string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(b))
            return Task.FromResult(0f);


        var normA = Normalise(a);
        var normB = Normalise(b);


        if (normA.Equals(normB, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return Task.FromResult(1f);


        int distance = LevenshteinDistance(normA, normB);
        int max = Math.Max(normA.Length, normB.Length);
        float similarity = 1f - ((float)distance / max);
        return Task.FromResult(similarity);
    }


    private static string Normalise(string input)
    {
        // Trim, collapse whitespace, lower‑case and expand a few abbreviations.
        var lowered = input.Trim().ToLowerInvariant();
        var collapsed = System.Text.RegularExpressions.Regex.Replace(lowered, @"\s+", " ");


        // Simple abbreviation map (extend as needed)
        var map = new (string Abbr, string Full)[]
        {
            ("st", "street"),
            ("ave", "avenue"),
            ("blvd", "boulevard"),
            ("rd", "road")
        };


        foreach (var (abbr, full) in map)
        {
            collapsed = collapsed.Replace(abbr, full);
        }


        return collapsed;
    }


    private static int LevenshteinDistance(string a, string b)
    {
        var costs = new int[b.Length + 1];


        for (int j = 0; j <= b.Length; j++) costs[j] = j;


        for (int i = 1; i <= a.Length; i++)
        {
            costs[0] = i;
            int corner = i - 1;


            for (int j = 1; j <= b.Length; j++)
            {
                int upper = costs[j];
                int cost = a[i - 1] == b[j - 1] ? 0 : 1;
                costs[j] = Math.Min(Math.Min(costs[j - 1] + 1, costs[j] + 1), corner + cost);
                corner = upper;
            }
        }


        return costs[b.Length];
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/TemporalMatcher.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic temporal similarity bucketed into ranges.
/// </summary>
public sealed class TemporalMatcher : ITemporalMatcher
{
    public Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(DateTimeOffset? a, DateTimeOffset? b)
    {
        if (!a.HasValue || !b.HasValue) return Task.FromResult(0f);


        var diff = Math.Abs((a.Value - b.Value).TotalMinutes);


        // Bucketed similarity – fully deterministic.
        // ≤ 5 min → 1.0, ≤ 15 min → 0.9, ≤ 30 min → 0.7,
        // ≤ 60 min → 0.5, ≤ 120 min → 0.3, > 2 h → 0.
        float similarity = diff switch
        {
            <= 5   => 1.0f,
            <= 15  => 0.9f,
            <= 30  => 0.7f,
            <= 60  => 0.5f,
            <= 120 => 0.3f,
            _      => 0f
        };
        return Task.FromResult(similarity);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/TemporalMatcher.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic temporal similarity bucketed into ranges.
/// </summary>
public sealed class TemporalMatcher : ITemporalMatcher
{
    public Task<float> ComputeSimilarityAsync(DateTimeOffset? a, DateTimeOffset? b)
    {
        if (!a.HasValue || !b.HasValue) return Task.FromResult(0f);


        var diff = Math.Abs((a.Value - b.Value).TotalMinutes);


        // Bucketed similarity – fully deterministic.
        // ≤ 5 min → 1.0, ≤ 15 min → 0.9, ≤ 30 min → 0.7,
        // ≤ 60 min → 0.5, ≤ 120 min → 0.3, > 2 h → 0.
        float similarity = diff switch
        {
            <= 5   => 1.0f,
            <= 15  => 0.9f,
            <= 30  => 0.7f,
            <= 60  => 0.5f,
            <= 120 => 0.3f,
            _      => 0f
        };
        return Task.FromResult(similarity);
    }
}


Rules & Constraints
* All matchers must be pure – no external I/O.
* They must return the same float for the same pair of inputs (deterministic).
* Normalisation is limited to whitespace, case, and a tiny abbreviation table (no external dictionaries).
Acceptance Criteria
1. IFuzzyStringMatcher and ITemporalMatcher interfaces compile.
2. LevenshteinStringMatcher implements IFuzzyStringMatcher with deterministic normalisation.
3. TemporalMatcher implements ITemporalMatcher with the minute‑bucket table shown.
4. Unit‑test placeholders (see M2‑P07 test file) verify:
   * Identical strings → 1.0.
   * “St” vs “Street” → > 0.9 after abbreviation expansion.
   * 3 minute time diff → 1.0; 45 minute diff → 0.5; > 2 h diff → 0.0.
5. Both implementations are registered in DI (e.g., services.AddTransient<IFuzzyStringMatcher, LevenshteinStringMatcher>();).

M2‑P08 — Implement Geo‑Spatial Proximity Matching v1.0
markdown
# Context
Geographic similarity is a key dimension for deduplication.  
We need a deterministic matcher that evaluates the distance between two `GeoPoint`s (or between a raw location string and a known `GeoPoint`) and returns a similarity score.


# Objective
Define the `IGeoMatcher` contract, a `GeoPoint` value object, and a concrete Haversine‑based implementation that produces a **0 – 1** similarity score using fixed distance buckets.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe/GeoMatchContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Simple value object that stores latitude/longitude in decimal degrees.
/// </summary>
public readonly record struct GeoPoint(double Latitude, double Longitude);


/// <summary>
/// Result returned by the geo matcher.
/// </summary>
public sealed record GeoMatchResult(
    float Similarity,          // 0.0 – 1.0
    double DistanceMeters);   // Raw distance for diagnostics


/// <summary>
/// Service that computes a similarity score between two geographic points.
/// </summary>
public interface IGeoMatcher
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Compute similarity between two points.  Returns 0 if either point is null.
    /// </summary>
    Task<GeoMatchResult> ComputeSimilarityAsync(GeoPoint? a, GeoPoint? b);
}


# Context
Geographic similarity is a key dimension for deduplication.  
We need a deterministic matcher that evaluates the distance between two `GeoPoint`s (or between a raw location string and a known `GeoPoint`) and returns a similarity score.


# Objective
Define the `IGeoMatcher` contract, a `GeoPoint` value object, and a concrete Haversine‑based implementation that produces a **0 – 1** similarity score using fixed distance buckets.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)


```csharp

namespace WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Simple value object that stores latitude/longitude in decimal degrees.
/// </summary>
public readonly record struct GeoPoint(double Latitude, double Longitude);


/// <summary>

/// </summary>
public sealed record GeoMatchResult(
    float Similarity,          // 0.0 – 1.0
    double DistanceMeters);   // Raw distance for diagnostics


/// <summary>
/// Service that computes a similarity score between two geographic points.
/// </summary>
public interface IGeoMatcher
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Compute similarity between two points.  Returns 0 if either point is null.
    /// </summary>
    Task<GeoMatchResult> ComputeSimilarityAsync(GeoPoint? a, GeoPoint? b);
}


Implementation (place in WeUP.Application/Dedupe/GeoMatcher.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/GeoMatcher.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic Haversine‑based matcher with fixed similarity buckets.
/// </summary>
public sealed class GeoMatcher : IGeoMatcher
{
    private readonly ILogger<GeoMatcher> _log;
    private const double EarthRadiusMeters = 6_371_000; // mean Earth radius


    public GeoMatcher(ILogger<GeoMatcher> log) => _log = log;


    public Task<GeoMatchResult> ComputeSimilarityAsync(GeoPoint? a, GeoPoint? b)
    {
        if (!a.HasValue || !b.HasValue)
            return Task.FromResult(new GeoMatchResult(0f, double.MaxValue));


        double meters = HaversineMeters(a.Value, b.Value);


        // Bucketed similarity – deterministic.
        // < 30 m → 1.0, < 100 m → 0.9, < 250 m → 0.8,
        // < 500 m → 0.6, < 1000 m → 0.4, < 2000 m → 0.2, else 0.
        float similarity = meters switch
        {
            < 30   => 1.0f,
            < 100  => 0.9f,
            < 250  => 0.8f,
            < 500  => 0.6f,
            < 1000 => 0.4f,
            < 2000 => 0.2f,
            _      => 0f
        };


        _log.LogDebug("Geo similarity: {Meters:N0} m → {Score}", meters, similarity);
        return Task.FromResult(new GeoMatchResult(similarity, meters));
    }


    // ---------- private helper ----------
    private static double ToRadians(double degrees) => degrees * Math.PI / 180.0;


    private static double HaversineMeters(GeoPoint a, GeoPoint b)
    {
        double dLat = ToRadians(b.Latitude - a.Latitude);
        double dLon = ToRadians(b.Longitude - a.Longitude);
        double lat1 = ToRadians(a.Latitude);
        double lat2 = ToRadians(b.Latitude);


        double h = Math.Sin(dLat / 2) * Math.Sin(dLat / 2) +
                   Math.Sin(dLon / 2) * Math.Sin(dLon / 2) *
                   Math.Cos(lat1) * Math.Cos(lat2);
        double c = 2 * Math.Atan2(Math.Sqrt(h), Math.Sqrt(1 - h));
        return EarthRadiusMeters * c;
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/GeoMatcher.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic Haversine‑based matcher with fixed similarity buckets.
/// </summary>
public sealed class GeoMatcher : IGeoMatcher
{
    private readonly ILogger<GeoMatcher> _log;
    private const double EarthRadiusMeters = 6_371_000; // mean Earth radius


    public GeoMatcher(ILogger<GeoMatcher> log) => _log = log;


    public Task<GeoMatchResult> ComputeSimilarityAsync(GeoPoint? a, GeoPoint? b)
    {
        if (!a.HasValue || !b.HasValue)
            return Task.FromResult(new GeoMatchResult(0f, double.MaxValue));


        double meters = HaversineMeters(a.Value, b.Value);


        // Bucketed similarity – deterministic.
        // < 30 m → 1.0, < 100 m → 0.9, < 250 m → 0.8,
        // < 500 m → 0.6, < 1000 m → 0.4, < 2000 m → 0.2, else 0.
        float similarity = meters switch
        {
            < 30   => 1.0f,
            < 100  => 0.9f,
            < 250  => 0.8f,
            < 500  => 0.6f,
            < 1000 => 0.4f,
            < 2000 => 0.2f,
            _      => 0f
        };


        _log.LogDebug("Geo similarity: {Meters:N0} m → {Score}", meters, similarity);
        return Task.FromResult(new GeoMatchResult(similarity, meters));
    }


    // ---------- private helper ----------
    private static double ToRadians(double degrees) => degrees * Math.PI / 180.0;


    private static double HaversineMeters(GeoPoint a, GeoPoint b)
    {
        double dLat = ToRadians(b.Latitude - a.Latitude);
        double dLon = ToRadians(b.Longitude - a.Longitude);
        double lat1 = ToRadians(a.Latitude);
        double lat2 = ToRadians(b.Latitude);


        double h = Math.Sin(dLat / 2) * Math.Sin(dLat / 2) +
                   Math.Sin(dLon / 2) * Math.Sin(dLon / 2) *
                   Math.Cos(lat1) * Math.Cos(lat2);
        double c = 2 * Math.Atan2(Math.Sqrt(h), Math.Sqrt(1 - h));
        return EarthRadiusMeters * c;
    }
}


Rules & Constraints
* Must be pure – no network calls, no caching.
* Distance calculation must use the standard Haversine formula (deterministic).
* Similarity is bucketed exactly as defined; no interpolation.
* The method must never throw – any error results in a similarity of 0.
Acceptance Criteria
1. GeoPoint, GeoMatchResult and IGeoMatcher compile.
2. GeoMatcher implements the bucketed similarity logic and logs the raw distance.
3. Unit‑test placeholders (see M2‑P08 test file) verify:
   * Identical points → similarity 1.0, distance 0.
   * 50 m apart → 0.9.
   * 1500 m apart → 0.2.
   * Null input → 0.0.
4. The matcher is registered in DI (services.AddTransient<IGeoMatcher, GeoMatcher>();).

M2‑P09 — Create Merge Planning and Conflict Resolution System v1.0
markdown
# Context
When two events are deemed *duplicates* we must decide **how to merge** them without losing provenance.  
The merge planner consumes a `DuplicateAssessment` and produces a plan that can be executed automatically, sent to moderation, or rejected.


# Objective
Define a **merge‑plan model**, field‑level actions, a planner service, and the accompanying EF Core entity for persisting the plan.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Dedupe/MergePlanContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Action to be taken for a single field during merge.
/// </summary>
public enum MergeFieldAction
{
    KeepExisting,        // Preserve the value already stored in the canonical event
    ReplaceWithCandidate,// Overwrite canonical value with the candidate's value
    MergeValues,          // Combine (e.g., concatenate tags)
    RequireManualReview   // Human must decide
}


/// <summary>
/// Description of a concrete conflict on a field.
/// </summary>
public sealed record ConflictDescriptor(
    string FieldName,
    string ExistingValue,
    string CandidateValue,
    string Reason);               // Human‑readable explanation


/// <summary>
/// Full merge plan for a candidate vs. a canonical event.
/// </summary>
public sealed record MergePlan(
    Guid CandidateId,
    Guid CanonicalEventId,
    MergeFieldAction TitleAction,
    MergeFieldAction LocationAction,
    MergeFieldAction TimeAction,
    MergeFieldAction TagsAction,
    ConflictDescriptor[] Conflicts,
    bool AutoApply);               // true → system can execute automatically

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Dedupe/IMergePlanner.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Service that turns a <see cref="DuplicateAssessment"/> into a concrete <see cref="MergePlan"/>.
/// </summary>
public interface IMergePlanner
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Generate a merge plan.  The method must be deterministic; given the same inputs
    /// it must always return the same plan.
    /// </summary>
    Task<MergePlan> GeneratePlanAsync(
        DuplicateAssessment assessment,
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        EventAggregate canonical);
}

using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Service that turns a <see cref="DuplicateAssessment"/> into a concrete <see cref="MergePlan"/>.
/// </summary>
public interface IMergePlanner
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Generate a merge plan.  The method must be deterministic; given the same inputs
    /// it must always return the same plan.
    /// </summary>
    Task<MergePlan> GeneratePlanAsync(
        DuplicateAssessment assessment,
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        EventAggregate canonical);
}


Reference implementation (place in WeUP.Application/Dedupe/MergePlanner.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/MergePlanner.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic merge planner – decides field actions based on similarity scores.
/// </summary>
public sealed class MergePlanner : IMergePlanner
{
    private readonly ILogger<MergePlanner> _log;
    private const float AutoMergeThreshold = 0.93f; // Above this we can auto‑apply


    public MergePlanner(ILogger<MergePlanner> log) => _log = log;


    public Task<MergePlan> GeneratePlanAsync(
        DuplicateAssessment assessment,
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        EventAggregate canonical)
    {
        // Resolve each dimension's score.
        var titleScore   = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.Title);
        var venueScore   = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.Venue);
        var timeScore    = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.StartTime);
        var locationScore= GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.Location);
        var hashScore    = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.SourceHash);


        var conflicts = new List<ConflictDescriptor>();


        // ---------- Title ----------
        var titleAction = ResolveAction(
            titleScore,
            candidate.Title,
            canonical.Title,
            "Title",
            out var titleConflict);
        if (titleConflict != null) conflicts.Add(titleConflict);


        // ---------- Location ----------
        var locationAction = ResolveAction(
            locationScore,
            candidate.RawLocationText,
            canonical.VenueName,
            "Location",
            out var locConflict);
        if (locConflict != null) conflicts.Add(locConflict);


        // ---------- Time ----------
        var timeAction = ResolveAction(
            timeScore,
            candidate.InferredStartUtc?.ToString("o") ?? string.Empty,
            canonical.StartUtc?.ToString("o") ?? string.Empty,
            "StartTime",
            out var timeConflict);
        if (timeConflict != null) conflicts.Add(timeConflict);


        // ---------- Tags (simple example – merge if similarity > 0.8) ----------
        // Assuming both have a comma‑separated tag string in RawFields["Tags"]
        candidate.RawFields.TryGetValue("Tags", out var candTags);
        canonical.Tags.TryGetValue("Tags", out var canTags); // EventAggregate.Tags is a dictionary for demo
        var tagsAction = MergeFieldAction.KeepExisting;
        if (!string.Equals(candTags, canTags, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            tagsAction = titleScore >= 0.85f ? MergeFieldAction.MergeValues : MergeFieldAction.RequireManualReview;
            if (tagsAction == MergeFieldAction.RequireManualReview)
            {
                conflicts.Add(new ConflictDescriptor(
                    "Tags",
                    canTags ?? "(none)",
                    candTags ?? "(none)",
                    "Tag sets differ and similarity below auto‑merge threshold"));
            }
        }


        // ---------- Auto‑apply decision ----------
        bool autoApply = assessment.Level switch
        {
            DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ExactDuplicate    => true,
            DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ProbableDuplicate => IsAboveThreshold(assessment),
            _                                          => false
        };


        var plan = new MergePlan(
            CandidateId: candidate.CandidateId,
            CanonicalEventId: canonical.EventId,
            TitleAction: titleAction,
            LocationAction: locationAction,
            TimeAction: timeAction,
            TagsAction: tagsAction,
            Conflicts: conflicts.ToArray(),
            AutoApply: autoApply);


        _log.LogInformation(
            "Merge plan generated for Candidate {CandidateId} vs Event {EventId} – AutoApply={Auto}",
            candidate.CandidateId, canonical.EventId, autoApply);


        return Task.FromResult(plan);
    }


    // ---------- helpers ----------
    private static float GetScore(MatchScoreBreakdown[] scores, MatchDimension dim)
        => scores.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Dimension == dim)?.Score ?? 0f;


    private static bool IsAboveThreshold(DuplicateAssessment assessment)
        => assessment.Scores.Average(s => s.Score) >= AutoMergeThreshold;


    private static MergeFieldAction ResolveAction(
        float similarity,
        string? candidateVal,
        string? existingVal,
        string fieldName,
        out ConflictDescriptor? conflict)
    {
        conflict = null;


        // Identical (or one side missing) → KeepExisting
        if (string.Equals(candidateVal?.Trim(), existingVal?.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return MergeFieldAction.KeepExisting;


        // High similarity → Replace
        if (similarity >= 0.90f)
            return MergeFieldAction.ReplaceWithCandidate;


        // Moderate similarity → MergeValues (e.g., concatenate tags)
        if (similarity >= 0.75f)
            return MergeFieldAction.MergeValues;


        // Low similarity → require manual review
        conflict = new ConflictDescriptor(
            FieldName: fieldName,
            ExistingValue: existingVal ?? "(null)",
            CandidateValue: candidateVal ?? "(null)",
            Reason: $"Similarity {similarity:P0} below threshold for automatic merge.");


        return MergeFieldAction.RequireManualReview;
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Dedupe/MergePlanner.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Dedupe;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Domain.Dedupe;


namespace WeUP.Application.Dedupe;


/// <summary>
/// Deterministic merge planner – decides field actions based on similarity scores.
/// </summary>
public sealed class MergePlanner : IMergePlanner
{
    private readonly ILogger<MergePlanner> _log;
    private const float AutoMergeThreshold = 0.93f; // Above this we can auto‑apply


    public MergePlanner(ILogger<MergePlanner> log) => _log = log;


    public Task<MergePlan> GeneratePlanAsync(
        DuplicateAssessment assessment,
        CandidateEvent candidate,
        EventAggregate canonical)
    {
        // Resolve each dimension's score.
        var titleScore   = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.Title);
        var venueScore   = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.Venue);
        var timeScore    = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.StartTime);
        var locationScore= GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.Location);
        var hashScore    = GetScore(assessment.Scores, MatchDimension.SourceHash);


        var conflicts = new List<ConflictDescriptor>();


        // ---------- Title ----------
        var titleAction = ResolveAction(
            titleScore,
            candidate.Title,
            canonical.Title,
            "Title",
            out var titleConflict);
        if (titleConflict != null) conflicts.Add(titleConflict);


        // ---------- Location ----------
        var locationAction = ResolveAction(
            locationScore,
            candidate.RawLocationText,
            canonical.VenueName,
            "Location",
            out var locConflict);
        if (locConflict != null) conflicts.Add(locConflict);


        // ---------- Time ----------
        var timeAction = ResolveAction(
            timeScore,
            candidate.InferredStartUtc?.ToString("o") ?? string.Empty,
            canonical.StartUtc?.ToString("o") ?? string.Empty,
            "StartTime",
            out var timeConflict);
        if (timeConflict != null) conflicts.Add(timeConflict);


        // ---------- Tags (simple example – merge if similarity > 0.8) ----------
        // Assuming both have a comma‑separated tag string in RawFields["Tags"]
        candidate.RawFields.TryGetValue("Tags", out var candTags);
        canonical.Tags.TryGetValue("Tags", out var canTags); // EventAggregate.Tags is a dictionary for demo
        var tagsAction = MergeFieldAction.KeepExisting;
        if (!string.Equals(candTags, canTags, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
        {
            tagsAction = titleScore >= 0.85f ? MergeFieldAction.MergeValues : MergeFieldAction.RequireManualReview;
            if (tagsAction == MergeFieldAction.RequireManualReview)
            {
                conflicts.Add(new ConflictDescriptor(
                    "Tags",
                    canTags ?? "(none)",
                    candTags ?? "(none)",
                    "Tag sets differ and similarity below auto‑merge threshold"));
            }
        }


        // ---------- Auto‑apply decision ----------
        bool autoApply = assessment.Level switch
        {
            DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ExactDuplicate    => true,
            DuplicateAssessmentLevel.ProbableDuplicate => IsAboveThreshold(assessment),
            _                                          => false
        };


        var plan = new MergePlan(
            CandidateId: candidate.CandidateId,
            CanonicalEventId: canonical.EventId,
            TitleAction: titleAction,
            LocationAction: locationAction,
            TimeAction: timeAction,
            TagsAction: tagsAction,
            Conflicts: conflicts.ToArray(),
            AutoApply: autoApply);


        _log.LogInformation(
            "Merge plan generated for Candidate {CandidateId} vs Event {EventId} – AutoApply={Auto}",
            candidate.CandidateId, canonical.EventId, autoApply);


        return Task.FromResult(plan);
    }


    // ---------- helpers ----------
    private static float GetScore(MatchScoreBreakdown[] scores, MatchDimension dim)
        => scores.FirstOrDefault(s => s.Dimension == dim)?.Score ?? 0f;


    private static bool IsAboveThreshold(DuplicateAssessment assessment)
        => assessment.Scores.Average(s => s.Score) >= AutoMergeThreshold;


    private static MergeFieldAction ResolveAction(
        float similarity,
        string? candidateVal,
        string? existingVal,
        string fieldName,
        out ConflictDescriptor? conflict)
    {
        conflict = null;


        // Identical (or one side missing) → KeepExisting
        if (string.Equals(candidateVal?.Trim(), existingVal?.Trim(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
            return MergeFieldAction.KeepExisting;


        // High similarity → Replace
        if (similarity >= 0.90f)
            return MergeFieldAction.ReplaceWithCandidate;


        // Moderate similarity → MergeValues (e.g., concatenate tags)
        if (similarity >= 0.75f)
            return MergeFieldAction.MergeValues;


        // Low similarity → require manual review
        conflict = new ConflictDescriptor(
            FieldName: fieldName,
            ExistingValue: existingVal ?? "(null)",
            CandidateValue: candidateVal ?? "(null)",
            Reason: $"Similarity {similarity:P0} below threshold for automatic merge.");


        return MergeFieldAction.RequireManualReview;
    }
}

Rules & Constraints
* The planner must not mutate any EventAggregate – it only produces an immutable MergePlan.
* All decisions are deterministic: same DuplicateAssessment → same MergePlan.
* If any field’s similarity is below 0.75 the action must be RequireManualReview and a ConflictDescriptor must be emitted.
* AutoApply is true only for ExactDuplicate or when the average similarity ≥ 0.93.
Acceptance Criteria
1. MergeFieldAction, ConflictDescriptor, MergePlan records compile.
2. IMergePlanner interface exists with the async method.
3. MergePlanner implements the deterministic logic shown, logs the plan, and respects the thresholds.
4. EF Core MergePlanEntity with JSONB ConflictsJson is defined and configured.
5. Unit‑test placeholders (see M2‑P09 test file) verify:
   * Exact duplicate → all KeepExisting, AutoApply = true.
   * Partial similarity → some ReplaceWithCandidate or MergeValues.
   * Low similarity → at least one ConflictDescriptor and AutoApply = false.
6. Service is registered (services.AddTransient<IMergePlanner, MergePlanner>();).

M2‑P10 — Enforce Provenance Preservation Across Merged Events v1.0
markdown
# Context
When a merge plan is executed, every field change must leave an **immutable provenance trail** so that auditors can reconstruct *who* contributed *what* and *when*.


# Objective
Define a **provenance model**, a service that records provenance entries for each merge operation, and the necessary EF Core entities.  
All writes are append‑only; no existing rows are ever updated.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Resolution`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Resolution/ProvenanceContracts.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Resolution;


/// <summary>
/// One immutable provenance record for a single field change during a merge.
/// </summary>
public sealed record ProvenanceEntry(
    Guid Id,
    Guid EventId,                 // Canonical event being modified
    Guid CandidateId,             // Source of the new data
    string FieldName,             // e.g., "Title", "StartUtc", "VenueName"
    string OldValue,
    string NewValue,
    DateTimeOffset ChangedAtUtc,
    string ChangedBy,              // Typically the system user “MergeEngine”
    string Reason);               // Human‑readable justification (e.g., "Auto‑merge high similarity")

When a merge plan is executed, every field change must leave an **immutable provenance trail** so that auditors can reconstruct *who* contributed *what* and *when*.


# Objective
Define a **provenance model**, a service that records provenance entries for each merge operation, and the necessary EF Core entities.  
All writes are append‑only; no existing rows are ever updated.

Interface (place in WeUP.Domain/Resolution)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Resolution/IProvenanceService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Resolution;
using WeUP.Domain.Events;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Resolution;


/// <summary>
/// Service responsible for persisting provenance entries in an append‑only fashion
/// and for retrieving the full lineage of an event.
/// </summary>
public interface IProvenanceService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Record a new provenance entry. Must be immutable – the implementation must never update existing rows.
    /// </summary>
    Task RecordAsync(ProvenanceEntry entry);


    /// <summary>
    /// Retrieve the complete provenance chain for a given <paramref name="eventId"/>.
    /// Results are ordered by <c>ChangedAtUtc</c> ascending.
    /// </summary>
    Task<IReadOnlyList<ProvenanceEntry>> GetLineageAsync(Guid eventId);
}

EF Configuration (place in WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/ProvenanceConfiguration.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/ProvenanceConfiguration.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Resolution;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Configurations;


public sealed class ProvenanceConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<ProvenanceEntity>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<ProvenanceEntity> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("Provenance");
        builder.HasKey(p => p.Id);
        builder.Property(p => p.FieldName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(p => p.ChangedBy).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(p => p.Reason).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(500);
        // Append‑only: prevent updates/deletes via EF.
        builder.Metadata.SetAfterSaveBehavior(PropertySaveBehavior.Throw);
    }
}

Service Implementation (place in WeUP.Application/Resolution/ProvenanceService.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Resolution/ProvenanceService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Resolution;
using WeUP.Domain.Resolution;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;


namespace WeUP.Application.Resolution;


/// <summary>
/// Append‑only provenance service.  All writes are performed via INSERT only.
/// </summary>
public sealed class ProvenanceService : IProvenanceService
{
    private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
    private readonly ILogger<ProvenanceService> _log;


    public ProvenanceService(WeUpDbContext db, ILogger<ProvenanceService> log)
    {
        _db = db;
        _log = log;
    }


    public async Task RecordAsync(ProvenanceEntry entry)
    {
        // Defensive: ensure no duplicate Ids.
        var exists = await _db.Provenance.AnyAsync(p => p.Id == entry.Id);
        if (exists)
        {
            _log.LogWarning("Duplicate provenance entry ignored: {Id}", entry.Id);
            return;
        }


        var entity = new ProvenanceEntity
        {
            Id = entry.Id,
            EventId = entry.EventId,
            CandidateId = entry.CandidateId,
            FieldName = entry.FieldName,
            OldValue = entry.OldValue,
            NewValue = entry.NewValue,
            ChangedAtUtc = entry.ChangedAtUtc,
            ChangedBy = entry.ChangedBy,
            Reason = entry.Reason
        };


        _db.Provenance.Add(entity);
        await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


        _log.LogInformation(
            "Provenance recorded: Event {EventId}, Field {Field}, From '{Old}' → '{New}'",
            entry.EventId, entry.FieldName, entry.OldValue, entry.NewValue);
    }


    public async Task<IReadOnlyList<ProvenanceEntry>> GetLineageAsync(Guid eventId)
    {
        var rows = await _db.Provenance
            .Where(p => p.EventId == eventId)
            .OrderBy(p => p.ChangedAtUtc)
            .ToListAsync();


        var result = rows.Select(p => new ProvenanceEntry(
            Id: p.Id,
            EventId: p.EventId,
            CandidateId: p.CandidateId,
            FieldName: p.FieldName,
            OldValue: p.OldValue,
            NewValue: p.NewValue,
            ChangedAtUtc: p.ChangedAtUtc,
            ChangedBy: p.ChangedBy,
            Reason: p.Reason)).ToArray();


        return result;
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Resolution/ProvenanceService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------

namespace WeUP.Application.Resolution;


/// <summary>

public sealed class ProvenanceService : IProvenanceService
{

    private readonly ILogger<ProvenanceService> _log;


    public ProvenanceService(WeUpDbContext db, ILogger<ProvenanceService> log)
    {
        _db = db;
        _log = log;
    }


    public async Task RecordAsync(ProvenanceEntry entry)
    {
        // Defensive: ensure no duplicate Ids.
        var exists = await _db.Provenance.AnyAsync(p => p.Id == entry.Id);
        if (exists)
        {
            _log.LogWarning("Duplicate provenance entry ignored: {Id}", entry.Id);
            return;
        }

        {
            Id = entry.Id,
            EventId = entry.EventId,

            NewValue: p.NewValue,

}


Rules & Constraints
* Append‑only – the EF Core configuration must reject any UPDATE or DELETE attempts (throw on save).
* Every merge operation (executed elsewhere) must call IProvenanceService.RecordAsync for each changed field.
* ProvenanceEntry.Id is a new GUID per record; duplicate Ids are ignored.
* The service does not perform any business logic – it merely persists the immutable record.
Acceptance Criteria
1. ProvenanceEntry record, IProvenanceService interface, and EF Core ProvenanceEntity compile.
2. ProvenanceService implementation:
   * Inserts a new row (no updates).
   * Logs each insertion.
   * Returns ordered lineage via GetLineageAsync.
3. EF Core configuration (ProvenanceConfiguration) enforces append‑only behavior (throws on accidental updates).
4. Unit‑test placeholders (see M2‑P10 test file) verify:
   * First insert succeeds, second insert with same Id is ignored.
   * Retrieval returns entries in chronological order.
   * Attempting to update an existing row via the DbContext throws an exception.
5. Service is registered in DI (services.AddTransient<IProvenanceService, ProvenanceService>();).

M3‑P11 — Define EventRiskScore and Moderation Classification Model v1.0
markdown
# Context
The moderation pipeline must decide **how risky** a newly‑created `CandidateEvent` is before it ever reaches the public map.  
Risk is derived from missing fields, low confidence, conflicting data and simple keyword heuristics.


# Objective
Create a deterministic risk‑scoring model that produces a **structured `EventRiskScore`** and an accompanying
`EventRiskLevel` enum.  Provide a pure service contract that can be implemented with rule‑based logic.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Moderation/EventRiskContracts.cs`)


```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Overall risk tier for a candidate event.
/// </summary>
public enum EventRiskLevel
{
    Low,        // Safe to auto‑publish
    Medium,     // Requires manual review
    High,       // Must be reviewed; likely problematic
    Restricted  // Auto‑reject unless overridden by a privileged moderator
}


/// <summary>
/// Detailed risk breakdown for a single candidate event.
/// </summary>
public sealed record EventRiskScore(
    Guid CandidateId,
    EventRiskLevel Level,
    float OverallScore,                         // 0.0 – 1.0 (higher = riskier)
    IReadOnlyDictionary<string, float> Factors, // e.g., "MissingTitle" → 0.9f
    string Explanation);                        // Human‑readable message


# Context
The moderation pipeline must decide **how risky** a newly‑created `CandidateEvent` is before it ever reaches the public map.  
Risk is derived from missing fields, low confidence, conflicting data and simple keyword heuristics.


# Objective
Create a deterministic risk‑scoring model that produces a **structured `EventRiskScore`** and an accompanying
`EventRiskLevel` enum.  Provide a pure service contract that can be implemented with rule‑based logic.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Moderation/EventRiskContracts.cs`)


```csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Overall risk tier for a candidate event.
/// </summary>
public enum EventRiskLevel
{
    Low,        // Safe to auto‑publish
    Medium,     // Requires manual review
    High,       // Must be reviewed; likely problematic
    Restricted  // Auto‑reject unless overridden by a privileged moderator
}


/// <summary>
/// Detailed risk breakdown for a single candidate event.
/// </summary>
public sealed record EventRiskScore(
    Guid CandidateId,
    EventRiskLevel Level,
    float OverallScore,                         // 0.0 – 1.0 (higher = riskier)
    IReadOnlyDictionary<string, float> Factors, // e.g., "MissingTitle" → 0.9f
    string Explanation);                        // Human‑readable message


Interface (place in WeUP.Domain/Moderation/IRiskScoringService.cs)
csharp
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Pure, deterministic service that computes an <see cref="EventRiskScore"/> for a candidate.
/// No external I/O may be performed; all logic must be rule‑based.
/// </summary>
public interface IRiskScoringService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Evaluate the risk of the supplied <paramref name="candidate"/>.
    /// </summary>
    Task<EventRiskScore> ComputeRiskAsync(CandidateEvent candidate);
}


using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Pure, deterministic service that computes an <see cref="EventRiskScore"/> for a candidate.
/// No external I/O may be performed; all logic must be rule‑based.
/// </summary>
public interface IRiskScoringService
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Evaluate the risk of the supplied <paramref name="candidate"/>.
    /// </summary>
    Task<EventRiskScore> ComputeRiskAsync(CandidateEvent candidate);
}


Reference implementation (place in WeUP.Application/Moderation/RiskScoringService.cs)
csharp
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;
using WeUP.Domain.Moderation;


namespace WeUP.Application.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Simple rule‑based risk scorer used for the MVP.  All thresholds are hard‑coded to keep the
/// implementation deterministic; they can be moved to configuration later.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RiskScoringService : IRiskScoringService
{
    private readonly ILogger<RiskScoringService> _log;


    // Hard‑coded thresholds (feel free to move to IOptions<> later)
    private const float MissingFieldPenalty   = 0.30f;
    private const float LowConfidencePenalty = 0.25f;
    private const float ConflictPenalty       = 0.20f;
    private const float KeywordPenalty       = 0.15f;


    // Keywords that raise suspicion (case‑insensitive)
    private static readonly string[] SuspiciousKeywords =
    {
        "free", "giveaway", "lottery", "spam", "adult", "drugs"
    };


    public RiskScoringService(ILogger<RiskScoringService> log) => _log = log;


    public Task<EventRiskScore> ComputeRiskAsync(CandidateEvent candidate)
    {
        var factors = new Dictionary<string, float>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        float score = 0f;
        var explanations = new List<string>();


        // ----- 1️⃣ Missing critical fields -----
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate.Title))
        {
            score += MissingFieldPenalty;
            factors["MissingTitle"] = MissingFieldPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Title is missing.");
        }


        if (!candidate.InferredStartUtc.HasValue)
        {
            score += MissingFieldPenalty;
            factors["MissingStartTime"] = MissingFieldPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Start time is missing.");
        }


        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate.RawLocationText))
        {
            score += MissingFieldPenalty;
            factors["MissingLocation"] = MissingFieldPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Location text is missing.");
        }


        // ----- 2️⃣ Low confidence from extraction -----
        if (candidate.OverallExtractionConfidence < 0.70f)
        {
            var penalty = LowConfidencePenalty * (1f - candidate.OverallExtractionConfidence);
            score += penalty;
            factors["LowConfidence"] = penalty;
            explanations.Add($"Overall confidence low ({candidate.OverallExtractionConfidence:P0}).");
        }


        // ----- 3️⃣ Keyword heuristics -----
        foreach (var kw in SuspiciousKeywords)
        {
            if ((candidate.Title?.Contains(kw, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false) ||
                (candidate.RawLocationText?.Contains(kw, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false))
            {
                score += KeywordPenalty;
                factors[$"Keyword:{kw}"] = KeywordPenalty;
                explanations.Add($"Suspicious keyword detected: \"{kw}\".");
            }
        }


        // ----- 4️⃣ Simple conflict detection (placeholder) -----
        // For MVP we just flag when the raw location looks like an address but the title contains a phone number.
        bool looksLikeAddress = candidate.RawLocationText?.Contains("St", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false;
        bool titleHasPhone = candidate.Title?.Any(char.IsDigit) ?? false; // crude check
        if (looksLikeAddress && titleHasPhone)
        {
            score += ConflictPenalty;
            factors["Location/PhoneConflict"] = ConflictPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Location appears to be an address while title contains a phone number – possible spam.");
        }


        // Clamp score to 0‑1 range.
        score = Math.Min(1f, Math.Max(0f, score));


        // Derive risk level from score.
        var level = score switch
        {
            >= 0.80f => EventRiskLevel.Restricted,
            >= 0.60f => EventRiskLevel.High,
            >= 0.40f => EventRiskLevel.Medium,
            _        => EventRiskLevel.Low
        };


        var result = new EventRiskScore(
            CandidateId: candidate.CandidateId,
            Level: level,
            OverallScore: score,
            Factors: factors,
            Explanation: string.Join(" ", explanations));


        _log.LogInformation(
            "RiskScore computed for Candidate {CandidateId}: Level={Level}, Score={Score:P2}",
            candidate.CandidateId, level, score);


        return Task.FromResult(result);
    }
}


using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Ingestion;
using WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;
using WeUP.Domain.Moderation;


namespace WeUP.Application.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Simple rule‑based risk scorer used for the MVP.  All thresholds are hard‑coded to keep the
/// implementation deterministic; they can be moved to configuration later.
/// </summary>
public sealed class RiskScoringService : IRiskScoringService
{
    private readonly ILogger<RiskScoringService> _log;


    // Hard‑coded thresholds (feel free to move to IOptions<> later)
    private const float MissingFieldPenalty   = 0.30f;
    private const float LowConfidencePenalty = 0.25f;
    private const float ConflictPenalty       = 0.20f;
    private const float KeywordPenalty       = 0.15f;


    // Keywords that raise suspicion (case‑insensitive)
    private static readonly string[] SuspiciousKeywords =
    {
        "free", "giveaway", "lottery", "spam", "adult", "drugs"
    };


    public RiskScoringService(ILogger<RiskScoringService> log) => _log = log;


    public Task<EventRiskScore> ComputeRiskAsync(CandidateEvent candidate)
    {
        var factors = new Dictionary<string, float>(StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase);
        float score = 0f;
        var explanations = new List<string>();


        // ----- 1️⃣ Missing critical fields -----
        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate.Title))
        {
            score += MissingFieldPenalty;
            factors["MissingTitle"] = MissingFieldPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Title is missing.");
        }


        if (!candidate.InferredStartUtc.HasValue)
        {
            score += MissingFieldPenalty;
            factors["MissingStartTime"] = MissingFieldPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Start time is missing.");
        }


        if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(candidate.RawLocationText))
        {
            score += MissingFieldPenalty;
            factors["MissingLocation"] = MissingFieldPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Location text is missing.");
        }


        // ----- 2️⃣ Low confidence from extraction -----
        if (candidate.OverallExtractionConfidence < 0.70f)
        {
            var penalty = LowConfidencePenalty * (1f - candidate.OverallExtractionConfidence);
            score += penalty;
            factors["LowConfidence"] = penalty;
            explanations.Add($"Overall confidence low ({candidate.OverallExtractionConfidence:P0}).");
        }


        // ----- 3️⃣ Keyword heuristics -----
        foreach (var kw in SuspiciousKeywords)
        {
            if ((candidate.Title?.Contains(kw, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false) ||
                (candidate.RawLocationText?.Contains(kw, StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false))
            {
                score += KeywordPenalty;
                factors[$"Keyword:{kw}"] = KeywordPenalty;
                explanations.Add($"Suspicious keyword detected: \"{kw}\".");
            }
        }


        // ----- 4️⃣ Simple conflict detection (placeholder) -----
        // For MVP we just flag when the raw location looks like an address but the title contains a phone number.
        bool looksLikeAddress = candidate.RawLocationText?.Contains("St", StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase) ?? false;
        bool titleHasPhone = candidate.Title?.Any(char.IsDigit) ?? false; // crude check
        if (looksLikeAddress && titleHasPhone)
        {
            score += ConflictPenalty;
            factors["Location/PhoneConflict"] = ConflictPenalty;
            explanations.Add("Location appears to be an address while title contains a phone number – possible spam.");
        }


        // Clamp score to 0‑1 range.
        score = Math.Min(1f, Math.Max(0f, score));


        // Derive risk level from score.
        var level = score switch
        {
            >= 0.80f => EventRiskLevel.Restricted,
            >= 0.60f => EventRiskLevel.High,
            >= 0.40f => EventRiskLevel.Medium,
            _        => EventRiskLevel.Low
        };


        var result = new EventRiskScore(
            CandidateId: candidate.CandidateId,
            Level: level,
            OverallScore: score,
            Factors: factors,
            Explanation: string.Join(" ", explanations));


        _log.LogInformation(
            "RiskScore computed for Candidate {CandidateId}: Level={Level}, Score={Score:P2}",
            candidate.CandidateId, level, score);


        return Task.FromResult(result);
    }
}


Rules & Constraints
* Pure – no DB or network access.
* The algorithm must be deterministic: same CandidateEvent → same EventRiskScore.
* All thresholds are hard‑coded (or injected via IOptions<>) – no randomisation.
* The Explanation field must contain all reasons that contributed to the score.
Acceptance Criteria
1. EventRiskLevel, EventRiskScore compile as immutable types.
2. IRiskScoringService interface exists with the exact async method.
3. RiskScoringService implements the rule‑based logic shown, logs the result, and never performs I/O.
4. Unit‑test skeleton (see WeUP.Tests/Moderation/RiskScoringServiceTests.cs) validates:
   * Missing title → MissingTitle factor present, risk ≥ Low.
   * Low overall confidence (< 0.7) adds the proper penalty.
   * Detects a suspicious keyword and raises the score.
5. Service is registered (services.AddTransient<IRiskScoringService, RiskScoringService>();).
________________


M3‑P12 — Build Moderation Queue and Review Workflow v1.0
markdown
# Context
After a candidate has been scored, it must be placed into a **moderation queue** where a human reviewer (or an automated policy) decides whether to publish, edit, or reject it.


# Objective
Define the queue item, its status lifecycle, and a service that:
* Enqueues new candidates.
* Retrieves the **next** item based on risk level and FIFO order.
* Allows assignment of a reviewer.
* Persists status transitions atomically.


# Contracts (place in `WeUP.Contracts/Moderation/ModerationQueueContracts.cs`)


```csharp
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Moderation;


/// <summary>
/// Status of a moderation queue item.  The order of declaration reflects the allowed progression.
/// </summary>
public enum ModerationStatus
{
    Pending,        // Enqueued, not yet claimed
    InReview,       // Assigned to a reviewer
    Approved,       // Passed moderation, eligible for publishing
    Rejected,       // Blocked – will not be published
    NeedsEdit,      // Reviewer requests edits before re‑queue
    Published       // Final state after publish gate passes (optional for audit)
}


/// <summary>
/// A single entry in the moderation queue.
/// </summary>
public sealed record ModerationQueueItem(
    Guid QueueItemId,
    Guid CandidateId,
    ModerationStatus Status,
    DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc,
    DateTimeOffset? AssignedAtUtc,
    Guid? ReviewerId,               // Null until a reviewer claims it
    string? ReviewerNotes);           // Optional free‑text notes from the reviewer


# Context
After a candidate has been scored, it must be placed into a **moderation queue** where a human reviewer (or an automated policy) decides whether to publish, edit, or reject it.


# Objective

* Enqueues new candidates.
* Retrieves the **next** item based on risk level and FIFO order.
