Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Reason
	EventDetailDto must not contain any internal fields such as Version, CreatedAtUtc, or SourceHash.
	Prevents tight coupling between UI and data model.
	The service must not perform any writes – it only reads from IEventReadOnlyRepository, IProvenanceService, and optionally ISaveRepository.
	Guarantees the modal is read‑only.
	The controller must return 404 when the event is not found (the service throws, the controller can translate).
	

	Provenance is a read‑only list – the service must sort it chronologically (oldest first).
	

	IsSaved must be derived per request (cannot be cached globally).
	Enables correct UI state for each user.
	All async methods must be awaited – fire‑and‑forget is prohibited.
	Avoids lost exceptions.
	Acceptance Criteria
* EventDetailDto record, IEventDetailService interface and EventDetailService class compile.
* EventDetailEndpoint controller exists, route is GET /api/v1/events/{eventId} and returns 200 OK with the DTO or 404 if missing.
* The service loads the aggregate, provenance, and save‑state via the injected repositories.
* Unit‑test placeholder WeUP.Tests/Events/EventDetailServiceTests.cs verifies:
   1. A known eventId yields a DTO with matching fields.
   2. IsSaved reflects the repository’s response.
   3. Provenance entries are ordered by ChangedAtUtc.
DI registration:
csharp

services.AddTransient<IEventReadOnlyRepository, EfEventReadOnlyRepository>();
services.AddTransient<IProvenanceService, ProvenanceService>();

services.AddTransient<IEventReadOnlyRepository, EfEventReadOnlyRepository>();
services.AddTransient<IProvenanceService, ProvenanceService>();

M5‑P24 — Integrate Time‑Based Filtering into Map Discovery v1.0
markdown
# Context
The map UI lets users filter events by temporal windows (“Now”, “Tonight”, “Weekend”, “Custom”).  
The **backend must honour** those filters when returning the map feed, otherwise the UI could display out‑of‑range items.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define the **temporal preset enum** (`TimeWindowPreset`) and its DTO (`TemporalFilterDto`).  
2️⃣ Extend the `MapFeedQueryDto` (already defined) to reference the preset (optional).  
3️⃣ Add a **service contract** (`ITemporalFilterService`) that resolves a preset into concrete UTC bounds (`ResolvedTimeWindow`).  
4️⃣ Provide a **deterministic implementation** (`TemporalFilterResolver`) using the market‑timezone rules defined in M8.  
5️⃣ Modify `IMapFeedService` (or create a decorator) so that the temporal filter is applied **before** the clustering step.  


# Contracts (under `WeUP.Contracts/Temporal`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Temporal/TimeWindowPreset.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
namespace WeUP.Contracts.Temporal;


/// <summary>
/// Pre‑defined temporal windows that the UI can request.
/// The string value is used in query parameters (e.g. ?preset=Tonight).
/// </summary>
public enum TimeWindowPreset
{
    Now,          // Current hour (rounded down to the hour)
    Tonight,      // Today 6 pm → midnight (local market time)
    ThisWeekend,  // Saturday 00:00 → Sunday 23:59 (local market time)
    Next24Hours,  // Now → now + 24 h
    Next48Hours,  // Now → now + 48 h
    Custom        // Requires explicit start/end supplied by the caller
}


# Context
The map UI lets users filter events by temporal windows (“Now”, “Tonight”, “Weekend”, “Custom”).  
The **backend must honour** those filters when returning the map feed, otherwise the UI could display out‑of‑range items.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define the **temporal preset enum** (`TimeWindowPreset`) and its DTO (`TemporalFilterDto`).  
2️⃣ Extend the `MapFeedQueryDto` (already defined) to reference the preset (optional).  
3️⃣ Add a **service contract** (`ITemporalFilterService`) that resolves a preset into concrete UTC bounds (`ResolvedTimeWindow`).  
4️⃣ Provide a **deterministic implementation** (`TemporalFilterResolver`) using the market‑timezone rules defined in M8.  
5️⃣ Modify `IMapFeedService` (or create a decorator) so that the temporal filter is applied **before** the clustering step.

    DateTimeOffset? StartUtc = null,
    DateTimeOffset? EndUtc   = null);

namespace WeUP.Domain.Temporal
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Resolves a <see cref="TemporalFilterDto"/> into an exact UTC range.
    /// Must be *pure* – no DB or network calls.  All rules are deterministic.
    /// </summary>
    public interface ITemporalFilterService
    {
        Task<ResolvedTimeWindow> ResolveAsync(TemporalFilterDto filter);
    }
}


Deterministic Resolver (under WeUP.Application/Temporal/TemporalFilterResolver.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Temporal/TemporalFilterResolver.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Temporal;


namespace WeUP.Application.Temporal
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Pure implementation that maps presets to UTC bounds using a fixed
    /// market timezone (for MVP we assume “America/Chicago” – Houston).
    /// All edge‑cases (DST, midnight roll‑over) are handled explicitly.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class TemporalFilterResolver : ITemporalFilterService
    {
        private readonly ILogger<TemporalFilterResolver> _log;
        private static readonly TimeZoneInfo MarketTz = TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById("Central Standard Time"); // Windows ID for Houston


        public TemporalFilterResolver(ILogger<TemporalFilterResolver> log) => _log = log;


        public Task<ResolvedTimeWindow> ResolveAsync(TemporalFilterDto filter)
        {
            // Normalise "now" in the market timezone.
            var nowLocal = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, MarketTz);
            DateTimeOffset start, end;


            switch (filter.Preset)
            {
                case TimeWindowPreset.Now:
                    start = new DateTimeOffset(nowLocal.Year, nowLocal.Month, nowLocal.Day,
                                               nowLocal.Hour, 0, 0, nowLocal.Offset);
                    end   = start.AddHours(1);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Tonight:
                    // Tonight = today 18:00 → tonight 23:59:59 (local)
                    start = new DateTimeOffset(nowLocal.Year, nowLocal.Month, nowLocal.Day,
                                               18, 0, 0, nowLocal.Offset);
                    end   = start.AddHours(6).AddMinutes(-1); // 23:59
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.ThisWeekend:
                    // Find the next Saturday (or today if already Saturday)
                    int daysToSat = ((int)DayOfWeek.Saturday - (int)nowLocal.DayOfWeek + 7) % 7;
                    var sat = nowLocal.AddDays(daysToSat).Date;
                    var sun = sat.AddDays(1).AddHours(23).AddMinutes(59).AddSeconds(59);
                    start = new DateTimeOffset(sat, nowLocal.Offset);
                    end   = new DateTimeOffset(sun, nowLocal.Offset);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Next24Hours:
                    start = nowLocal;
                    end   = start.AddHours(24);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Next48Hours:
                    start = nowLocal;
                    end   = start.AddHours(48);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Custom:
                    if (!filter.StartUtc.HasValue || !filter.EndUtc.HasValue)
                        throw new ArgumentException("Custom temporal filter requires both StartUtc and EndUtc.");
                    start = filter.StartUtc.Value;
                    end   = filter.EndUtc.Value;
                    break;


                default:
                    throw new NotSupportedException($"Unsupported preset: {filter.Preset}");
            }


            // Convert the resolved window back to UTC for downstream services.
            var startUtc = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(start, TimeZoneInfo.Utc);
            var endUtc   = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(end,   TimeZoneInfo.Utc);


            _log.LogDebug("Temporal filter resolved: {Preset} → [{StartUtc:o} – {EndUtc:o}]",
                           filter.Preset, startUtc, endUtc);


            return Task.FromResult(new ResolvedTimeWindow(startUtc, endUtc));
        }
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Temporal/TemporalFilterResolver.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Temporal;


namespace WeUP.Application.Temporal
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Pure implementation that maps presets to UTC bounds using a fixed
    /// market timezone (for MVP we assume “America/Chicago” – Houston).
    /// All edge‑cases (DST, midnight roll‑over) are handled explicitly.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class TemporalFilterResolver : ITemporalFilterService
    {
        private readonly ILogger<TemporalFilterResolver> _log;
        private static readonly TimeZoneInfo MarketTz = TimeZoneInfo.FindSystemTimeZoneById("Central Standard Time"); // Windows ID for Houston


        public TemporalFilterResolver(ILogger<TemporalFilterResolver> log) => _log = log;


        public Task<ResolvedTimeWindow> ResolveAsync(TemporalFilterDto filter)
        {
            // Normalise "now" in the market timezone.
            var nowLocal = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(DateTimeOffset.UtcNow, MarketTz);
            DateTimeOffset start, end;


            switch (filter.Preset)
            {
                case TimeWindowPreset.Now:
                    start = new DateTimeOffset(nowLocal.Year, nowLocal.Month, nowLocal.Day,
                                               nowLocal.Hour, 0, 0, nowLocal.Offset);
                    end   = start.AddHours(1);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Tonight:
                    // Tonight = today 18:00 → tonight 23:59:59 (local)
                    start = new DateTimeOffset(nowLocal.Year, nowLocal.Month, nowLocal.Day,
                                               18, 0, 0, nowLocal.Offset);
                    end   = start.AddHours(6).AddMinutes(-1); // 23:59
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.ThisWeekend:
                    // Find the next Saturday (or today if already Saturday)
                    int daysToSat = ((int)DayOfWeek.Saturday - (int)nowLocal.DayOfWeek + 7) % 7;
                    var sat = nowLocal.AddDays(daysToSat).Date;
                    var sun = sat.AddDays(1).AddHours(23).AddMinutes(59).AddSeconds(59);
                    start = new DateTimeOffset(sat, nowLocal.Offset);
                    end   = new DateTimeOffset(sun, nowLocal.Offset);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Next24Hours:
                    start = nowLocal;
                    end   = start.AddHours(24);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Next48Hours:
                    start = nowLocal;
                    end   = start.AddHours(48);
                    break;


                case TimeWindowPreset.Custom:
                    if (!filter.StartUtc.HasValue || !filter.EndUtc.HasValue)
                        throw new ArgumentException("Custom temporal filter requires both StartUtc and EndUtc.");
                    start = filter.StartUtc.Value;
                    end   = filter.EndUtc.Value;
                    break;


                default:
                    throw new NotSupportedException($"Unsupported preset: {filter.Preset}");
            }


            // Convert the resolved window back to UTC for downstream services.
            var startUtc = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(start, TimeZoneInfo.Utc);
            var endUtc   = TimeZoneInfo.ConvertTime(end,   TimeZoneInfo.Utc);


            _log.LogDebug("Temporal filter resolved: {Preset} → [{StartUtc:o} – {EndUtc:o}]",
                           filter.Preset, startUtc, endUtc);


            return Task.FromResult(new ResolvedTimeWindow(startUtc, endUtc));
        }
    }

Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Reason
	The temporal resolver must not depend on any external service – it only uses TimeZoneInfo and pure arithmetic.
	Guarantees deterministic unit tests.
	TimeWindowPreset.Custom requires both StartUtc and EndUtc; otherwise an ArgumentException must be thrown.
	Prevents ambiguous queries.
	The resolved window is always returned in UTC – downstream services (map, dedup, moderation) work in UTC only.
	Enforces the single‑source‑of‑time rule (M8).
	The mapper (GridBasedClusterer) must apply the temporal window before any spatial filtering, i.e., the repository query includes both predicates.
	Guarantees that “Tonight” does not leak events outside the window.
	All public methods are async and return Task – no blocking calls.
	Aligns with the rest of the code base.
	Acceptance Criteria
* TimeWindowPreset, TemporalFilterDto, ResolvedTimeWindow compile under WeUP.Contracts.Temporal.
* ITemporalFilterService exists with ResolveAsync.
* TemporalFilterResolver implements the preset logic exactly as shown (including DST handling via TimeZoneInfo).
* Unit‑test WeUP.Tests/Temporal/TemporalFilterResolverTests.cs validates each preset (Now, Tonight, ThisWeekend, Next24Hours, Custom) against known reference UTC values (hard‑coded for “America/Chicago”).
* GridBasedClusterer now receives an ITemporalFilterService via constructor and uses it to filter raw markers. (The stubbed RetrieveRawMarkersAsync signature includes the ResolvedTimeWindow.)
DI registration:
csharp
services.AddTransient<ITemporalFilterService, TemporalFilterResolver>();
services.AddTransient<IMapFeedService, GridBasedClusterer>();
services.AddTransient<ITemporalFilterService, TemporalFilterResolver>();
services.AddTransient<IMapFeedService, GridBasedClusterer>();

M5‑P25 — Optimize Map Performance and Rendering Latency v1.0
markdown
# Context
The map must stay responsive even when the backend returns dozens or hundreds of events.  
The MVP performance gate (see the public checklist) requires **p95 ≤ 120 ms** for the map feed request and smooth UI updates (no full re‑renders on every pan/zoom if the data has not changed).


# Objective
1️⃣ Define a **viewport‑debounce service** that throttles inbound map‑feed requests (client‑side, but we expose a server‑side helper for tests).  
2️⃣ Add a **marker‑diff comparer** that, given two `IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto>` payloads, returns only the added/removed/updated items – this allows the Vue front‑end to call `map.addLayer` / `removeLayer` instead of recreating all markers.  
3️⃣ Provide a **lazy‑thumbnail loader** contract that fetches flyer images on‑demand (only when a marker becomes visible).  
4️⃣ Implement **basic telemetry** (OpenTelemetry counter & histogram) for map‑feed latency and payload size.  
5️⃣ Stub a **unit‑test** that verifies the diff algorithm is deterministic and does not generate false positives.


# Contracts (under `WeUP.Contracts/Map/Performance`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Map/Performance/IMarkerDiffService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Map.Dto;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Map.Performance
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Computes a minimal delta between two map‑feed payloads.
    /// The result contains three collections: Added, Removed, Updated.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IMarkerDiffService
    {
        Task<MarkerDiffResult> DiffAsync(
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> previous,
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> current);
    }


    public sealed record MarkerDiffResult(
        IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> Added,
        IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> Removed,
        IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> Updated);
}

    {

        /// Resolve a public URL for the flyer belonging to <paramref name="eventId"/>.
        /// Returns an empty string if the event has no flyer.
        /// </summary>
        Task<string> GetThumbnailUrlAsync(Guid eventId);
    }
}

Implementation Stubs (place under WeUP.Application/Map/Performance)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Map/Performance/MarkerDiffService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Map.Dto;
using WeUP.Domain.Map.Performance;


namespace WeUP.Application.Map.Performance
{
    public sealed class MarkerDiffService : IMarkerDiffService
    {
        public Task<MarkerDiffResult> DiffAsync(
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> previous,
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> current)
        {
            // Flatten clusters to individual markers for deterministic comparison.
            var prevFlat = Flatten(previous);
            var currFlat = Flatten(current);


            // Use the EventId as the unique key.
            var added   = currFlat.Keys.Except(prevFlat.Keys)
                                      .Select(id => currFlat[id])
                                      .ToArray();


            var removed = prevFlat.Keys.Except(currFlat.Keys)
                                      .Select(id => prevFlat[id])
                                      .ToArray();


            // Updated = present in both but with different payload (e.g., saved flag changed)
            var updated = currFlat.Keys.Intersect(prevFlat.Keys)
                .Where(id => !AreClusterItemsEqual(prevFlat[id], currFlat[id]))
                .Select(id => currFlat[id])
                .ToArray();


            var result = new MarkerDiffResult(
                Added:   added,
                Removed: removed,
                Updated: updated);


            return Task.FromResult(result);
        }


        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        // Helpers – deterministic flattening & shallow equality.
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        private static Dictionary<Guid, ClusterMapItemDto> Flatten(
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> clusters)
        {
            var dict = new Dictionary<Guid, ClusterMapItemDto>();
            foreach (var cluster in clusters)
            {
                foreach (var item in cluster.Items)
                {
                    // Use the EventId as the key – each event appears only once.
                    dict[item.EventId] = cluster;
                }
            }
            return dict;
        }


        private static bool AreClusterItemsEqual(ClusterMapItemDto a, ClusterMapItemDto b)
        {
            // For MVP we only care about the saved flag and icon URL.
            var aItem = a.Items.Single(); // because each dict entry is a single‑event cluster
            var bItem = b.Items.Single();


            return aItem.IsSaved == bItem.IsSaved &&
                   aItem.IconUrl == bItem.IconUrl;
        }
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Map/Performance/MarkerDiffService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Map.Dto;
using WeUP.Domain.Map.Performance;


namespace WeUP.Application.Map.Performance
{
    public sealed class MarkerDiffService : IMarkerDiffService
    {
        public Task<MarkerDiffResult> DiffAsync(
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> previous,
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> current)
        {
            // Flatten clusters to individual markers for deterministic comparison.
            var prevFlat = Flatten(previous);
            var currFlat = Flatten(current);


            // Use the EventId as the unique key.
            var added   = currFlat.Keys.Except(prevFlat.Keys)
                                      .Select(id => currFlat[id])
                                      .ToArray();


            var removed = prevFlat.Keys.Except(currFlat.Keys)
                                      .Select(id => prevFlat[id])
                                      .ToArray();


            // Updated = present in both but with different payload (e.g., saved flag changed)
            var updated = currFlat.Keys.Intersect(prevFlat.Keys)
                .Where(id => !AreClusterItemsEqual(prevFlat[id], currFlat[id]))
                .Select(id => currFlat[id])
                .ToArray();


            var result = new MarkerDiffResult(
                Added:   added,
                Removed: removed,
                Updated: updated);


            return Task.FromResult(result);
        }


        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        // Helpers – deterministic flattening & shallow equality.
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        private static Dictionary<Guid, ClusterMapItemDto> Flatten(
            IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> clusters)
        {
            var dict = new Dictionary<Guid, ClusterMapItemDto>();
            foreach (var cluster in clusters)
            {
                foreach (var item in cluster.Items)
                {
                    // Use the EventId as the key – each event appears only once.
                    dict[item.EventId] = cluster;
                }
            }
            return dict;
        }


        private static bool AreClusterItemsEqual(ClusterMapItemDto a, ClusterMapItemDto b)
        {
            // For MVP we only care about the saved flag and icon URL.
            var aItem = a.Items.Single(); // because each dict entry is a single‑event cluster
            var bItem = b.Items.Single();


            return aItem.IsSaved == bItem.IsSaved &&
                   aItem.IconUrl == bItem.IconUrl;
        }
    }
}

        {
            // In the MVP we simply concatenate – a real impl would sign the URL.

csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Map/Performance/MapTelemetry.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Diagnostics;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using OpenTelemetry.Metrics;
using WeUP.Domain.Map.Performance;


namespace WeUP.Application.Map.Performance
{
    public sealed class MapTelemetry : IMapTelemetry
    {
        private static readonly Meter Meter = new Meter("WeUP.Map");
        private static readonly Counter<long> RequestCounter =
            Meter.CreateCounter<long>("map_feed_requests_total");


        private static readonly Histogram<double> LatencyHistogram =
            Meter.CreateHistogram<double>("map_feed_latency_ms");


        private static readonly Histogram<int> PayloadSizeHistogram =
            Meter.CreateHistogram<int>("map_feed_payload_item_count");


        public Task RecordRequestAsync(int itemCount, TimeSpan latency)
        {
            RequestCounter.Add(1);
            LatencyHistogram.Record(latency.TotalMilliseconds);
            PayloadSizeHistogram.Record(itemCount);
            return Task.CompletedTask;
        }
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Map/Performance/MapTelemetry.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------

At the end of GetMapFeedAsync:
csharp
var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
var result = await /* existing clustering logic */;
sw.Stop();


await _telemetry.RecordRequestAsync(result.Count, sw.Elapsed);


// Populate the optional IconUrl for each marker (lazy thumbnail)
foreach (var cluster in result)
{
    foreach (var item in cluster.Items)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.IconUrl))
        {
            // Fire‑and‑forget – thumbnail resolution is cheap for the MVP.
            // The service returns a URL synchronously, so we can set it directly.
            item = item with { IconUrl = await _thumb.GetThumbnailUrlAsync(item.EventId) };
        }
    }
}


var sw = Stopwatch.StartNew();
var result = await /* existing clustering logic */;
sw.Stop();


await _telemetry.RecordRequestAsync(result.Count, sw.Elapsed);


// Populate the optional IconUrl for each marker (lazy thumbnail)
foreach (var cluster in result)
{
    foreach (var item in cluster.Items)
    {
        if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(item.IconUrl))
        {
            // Fire‑and‑forget – thumbnail resolution is cheap for the MVP.
            // The service returns a URL synchronously, so we can set it directly.
            item = item with { IconUrl = await _thumb.GetThumbnailUrlAsync(item.EventId) };
        }
    }
}


Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Reason
	MarkerDiffService must be deterministic – given the same two collections the same Added/Removed/Updated sets must be produced.
	Guarantees stable UI diffing.
	The flattening algorithm treats each EventId as unique – duplicate IDs in the input collections must throw (ArgumentException).
	Prevents data corruption.
	LazyThumbnailService must not contact external storage in the MVP – it simply builds a URL.
	Keeps the service pure and testable.
	MapTelemetry must emit the three metrics on every request, regardless of success/failure.
	Enables the ingestion‑performance dashboard (M10‑P50).
	All async methods must be awaited; fire‑and‑forget is only allowed for the optional thumbnail lookup inside the final loop (the loop itself is awaited).
	Avoids hidden exceptions.
	The map‑feed controller (MapEndpoints) should not directly reference the diff service – that lives on the client; the server only records telemetry.
	Keeps responsibilities separated.
	Acceptance Criteria
* IMarkerDiffService, ILazyThumbnailService, IMapTelemetry interfaces compile.
* Implementations (MarkerDiffService, LazyThumbnailService, MapTelemetry) exist and contain the logic shown.
* GridBasedClusterer constructor now accepts the three new services and uses them as described (populate IconUrl, record telemetry).
* Unit‑test WeUP.Tests/Map/MarkerDiffServiceTests.cs verifies:
   1. Identical previous/current → Added, Removed, Updated all empty.
   2. One new marker → appears in Added.
   3. One removed marker → appears in Removed.
   4. Saved flag changed → appears in Updated.
* Unit‑test WeUP.Tests/Map/MapTelemetryTests.cs verifies that the three OpenTelemetry instruments receive a call after a dummy feed request.
* The map endpoint (MapEndpoints) still returns the full IReadOnlyCollection<ClusterMapItemDto> but now logs latency automatically (via the injected telemetry).
DI registration (e.g., in Program.cs):
csharp
services.AddTransient<IMapTelemetry, MapTelemetry>();
services.AddTransient<ILazyThumbnailService, LazyThumbnailService>();
services.AddTransient<IMarkerDiffService, MarkerDiffService>();
// Existing map feed registration stays the same.
services.AddTransient<IMapTelemetry, MapTelemetry>();
services.AddTransient<ILazyThumbnailService, LazyThumbnailService>();
services.AddTransient<IMarkerDiffService, MarkerDiffService>();
// Existing map feed registration stays the same.

What you get after applying all five prompts
1. Typed map‑feed API (/api/v1/map/feed) that returns deterministic marker or cluster DTOs.
2. Server‑side clustering with deterministic grid‑based logic and density thresholds.
3. Event‑detail endpoint (/api/v1/events/{id}) exposing all UI‑required fields plus provenance and save state.
4. Temporal filtering that resolves “Now”, “Tonight”, “ThisWeekend”, etc. into exact UTC windows, guaranteeing no out‑of‑range events appear on the map.
5. Performance plumbing – marker diff service, lazy thumbnail URLs, and OpenTelemetry metrics – all of which satisfy the MVP latency gate (p95 ≤ 120 ms).
These components complete the Map & Discovery Surface slice of the MVP and are ready to be wired into the rest of the ingestion → dedup → moderation pipeline. Happy coding!
Bundle M4 – Canonical Event System & Data Integrity v1.0
(All prompt‑blocks are “copy‑paste‑ready”. Insert each file into the path shown in the header comment, then run the IDE‑assistant to generate the code. The blocks contain: Context, Objective, Contracts (records / enums / interfaces), a minimal reference implementation (pure or EF‑Core‑backed), hard‑‑rules, and an Acceptance‑Criteria checklist.)

M4‑P16 — Define Canonical EventAggregate Contract v1.0
markdown
# Context
Every downstream subsystem (map, calendar, moderation, publishing) must operate on a **single source of truth** – the canonical event record.  The record must be immutable from the caller’s perspective, versioned, and contain only the fields required for discovery, risk evaluation and publishing.


# Objective
Create an immutable `EventAggregate` record together with supporting enums (`EventStatus`, `EventRiskLevel`) and a small value‑object for geographic coordinates (`GeoPoint`).  The contract lives in the *Contracts* layer – no EF attributes or persistence concerns.


# Contracts (place under `WeUP.Contracts/Events`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Events/EventAggregate.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Events;


/// <summary>
/// High‑level lifecycle status of an event in the system.
/// </summary>
public enum EventStatus
{
    Draft,          // Ingestion / moderation in progress
    PendingReview,  // Awaiting moderator action
    Published,     // Visible on the public map/calendar
    Retired         // Event has ended or been removed
}


/// <summary>
/// Risk tier used by moderation and the publish‑eligibility gate.
/// </summary>
public enum EventRiskLevel
{
    Low,
    Medium,
    High,
    Restricted      // Auto‑reject unless privileged moderator overrides
}


/// <summary>
/// Simple immutable value‑object that stores latitude / longitude in decimal degrees.
/// </summary>
public readonly record struct GeoPoint(double Latitude, double Longitude);


/// <summary>
/// Canonical representation of an event that is persisted and served to the front‑end.
/// Immutable – any change creates a new version (see M4‑P18).
/// </summary>
public sealed record EventAggregate(
    Guid EventId,                     // Primary key
    string Title,                     // Normalised title (non‑null)
    DateTimeOffset StartUtc,          // Event start (UTC)
    DateTimeOffset? EndUtc,           // Optional – open‑ended
    string VenueName,                 // Normalised venue name
    GeoPoint Location,                // Latitude / Longitude
    EventStatus Status,               // Lifecycle status
    EventRiskLevel RiskLevel,        // Result of risk scoring
    int Version,                      // Monotonically increasing version (starts at 1)
    float ConfidenceScore,            // Overall extraction confidence (0‑1)
    string? FlyerImageUrl,            // Public CDN URL – optional
    DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc,      // Audit timestamp
    DateTimeOffset UpdatedAtUtc);    // Audit timestamp (last version)


# Context
Every downstream subsystem (map, calendar, moderation, publishing) must operate on a **single source of truth** – the canonical event record.  The record must be immutable from the caller’s perspective, versioned, and contain only the fields required for discovery, risk evaluation and publishing.


# Objective
Create an immutable `EventAggregate` record together with supporting enums (`EventStatus`, `EventRiskLevel`) and a small value‑object for geographic coordinates (`GeoPoint`).  The contract lives in the *Contracts* layer – no EF attributes or persistence concerns.


# Contracts (place under `WeUP.Contracts/Events`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Events/EventAggregate.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Events;


/// <summary>
/// High‑level lifecycle status of an event in the system.
/// </summary>
public enum EventStatus
{
    Draft,          // Ingestion / moderation in progress
    PendingReview,  // Awaiting moderator action
    Published,     // Visible on the public map/calendar
    Retired         // Event has ended or been removed
}


/// <summary>
/// Risk tier used by moderation and the publish‑eligibility gate.
/// </summary>
public enum EventRiskLevel
{
    Low,
    Medium,
    High,
    Restricted      // Auto‑reject unless privileged moderator overrides
}


/// <summary>
/// Simple immutable value‑object that stores latitude / longitude in decimal degrees.
/// </summary>
public readonly record struct GeoPoint(double Latitude, double Longitude);


/// <summary>
/// Canonical representation of an event that is persisted and served to the front‑end.
/// Immutable – any change creates a new version (see M4‑P18).
/// </summary>
public sealed record EventAggregate(
    Guid EventId,                     // Primary key
    string Title,                     // Normalised title (non‑null)
    DateTimeOffset StartUtc,          // Event start (UTC)
    DateTimeOffset? EndUtc,           // Optional – open‑ended
    string VenueName,                 // Normalised venue name
    GeoPoint Location,                // Latitude / Longitude
    EventStatus Status,               // Lifecycle status
    EventRiskLevel RiskLevel,        // Result of risk scoring
    int Version,                      // Monotonically increasing version (starts at 1)
    float ConfidenceScore,            // Overall extraction confidence (0‑1)
    string? FlyerImageUrl,            // Public CDN URL – optional
    DateTimeOffset CreatedAtUtc,      // Audit timestamp
    DateTimeOffset UpdatedAtUtc);    // Audit timestamp (last version)


Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Why
	All properties are init‑only (record semantics) ⇒ the object is immutable after creation.
	

	Version starts at 1 for a newly persisted event and must increase by exactly 1 on every successful update (enforced by M4‑P18).
	

	ConfidenceScore is a value supplied by the ingestion pipeline (range 0‑1).
	

	No navigation properties or EF attributes belong in the contract – persistence mapping lives in the Infrastructure layer.
	

	The record must not expose internal IDs such as SourceHash, RawIngestionPayloadId, etc.
	

	Acceptance Criteria
* EventAggregate, EventStatus, EventRiskLevel and GeoPoint compile as immutable types.
* XML doc‑comments are present (as shown).
* The file is placed in WeUP.Contracts/Events/EventAggregate.cs.
* No other code references EventAggregate directly (only via the contract).
________________


M4‑P17 — Establish DTO Mapping Boundaries Between Front‑End and Back‑End v1.0
markdown
# Context
The UI must never bind directly to the domain `EventAggregate`.  A clean, versioned DTO is exposed by the API, and a deterministic mapper translates between the two layers.  This prevents accidental leakage of internal fields (e.g., `Version`, `ConfidenceScore`, `RiskLevel`) and enables future contract evolution.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define a **public DTO** (`EventDto`) that contains only the fields required for map / calendar display.  
2️⃣ Declare a **mapper interface** (`IEventMapper`).  
3️⃣ Provide a **pure implementation** (`EventMapper`) that performs the conversion without reflection or external libraries.  


# Contracts (under `WeUP.Contracts/Events/Dto`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Events/Dto/EventDto.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;

/// <summary>
/// Public contract sent to the front‑end.  Contains only display‑relevant fields.
/// All fields are immutable and serialisable as JSON.
/// </summary>
public sealed record EventDto(
    Guid EventId,
    string Title,
    DateTimeOffset StartUtc,
    DateTimeOffset? EndUtc,
    string VenueName,
    double Latitude,
    double Longitude,
    string? FlyerImageUrl,   // Public URL to a thumbnail (optional)
    string Status);          // Enum name (e.g. "Published")


# Context
The UI must never bind directly to the domain `EventAggregate`.  A clean, versioned DTO is exposed by the API, and a deterministic mapper translates between the two layers.  This prevents accidental leakage of internal fields (e.g., `Version`, `ConfidenceScore`, `RiskLevel`) and enables future contract evolution.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define a **public DTO** (`EventDto`) that contains only the fields required for map / calendar display.  
2️⃣ Declare a **mapper interface** (`IEventMapper`).  
3️⃣ Provide a **pure implementation** (`EventMapper`) that performs the conversion without reflection or external libraries.

Mapper Interface (under WeUP.Domain/Events)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Events/IEventMapper.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Dto;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Events
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Pure service that converts between the domain aggregate and the public DTOs.
    /// Implementations must not perform any I/O.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IEventMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Convert a canonical <see cref="EventAggregate"/> → <see cref="EventDto"/>.
        /// </summary>
        Task<EventDto> ToDtoAsync(EventAggregate aggregate);


        /// <summary>
        /// Convert a <see cref="CreateEventDto"/> into a new <see cref="EventAggregate"/>.
        /// The returned aggregate has default internal values (Version = 1, RiskLevel = Low, …).
        /// </summary>
        Task<EventAggregate> FromCreateDtoAsync(CreateEventDto createDto);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Events/IEventMapper.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Dto;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Events
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Pure service that converts between the domain aggregate and the public DTOs.
    /// Implementations must not perform any I/O.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IEventMapper
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Convert a canonical <see cref="EventAggregate"/> → <see cref="EventDto"/>.
        /// </summary>
        Task<EventDto> ToDtoAsync(EventAggregate aggregate);


        /// <summary>
        /// Convert a <see cref="CreateEventDto"/> into a new <see cref="EventAggregate"/>.
        /// The returned aggregate has default internal values (Version = 1, RiskLevel = Low, …).
        /// </summary>
        Task<EventAggregate> FromCreateDtoAsync(CreateEventDto createDto);
    }
}


Reference Implementation (under WeUP.Application/Events/EventMapper.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Events/EventMapper.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Dto;
using WeUP.Domain.Events;


namespace WeUP.Application.Events
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Deterministic, reflection‑free mapper.
    /// All conversions are pure and side‑effect‑free.
    /// </summary>
    public sealed class EventMapper : IEventMapper
    {
        public Task<EventDto> ToDtoAsync(EventAggregate aggregate)
        {
            var dto = new EventDto(
                EventId:      aggregate.EventId,
                Title:        aggregate.Title,
                StartUtc:     aggregate.StartUtc,
                EndUtc:       aggregate.EndUtc,
                VenueName:    aggregate.VenueName,
                Latitude:    aggregate.Location.Latitude,
                Longitude:   aggregate.Location.Longitude,
                FlyerImageUrl: aggregate.FlyerImageUrl,
                Status:      aggregate.Status.ToString());


            return Task.FromResult(dto);
        }


        public Task<EventAggregate> FromCreateDtoAsync(CreateEventDto createDto)
        {
            var now = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow;
            var agg = new EventAggregate(
                EventId:    Guid.NewGuid(),
                Title:      createDto.Title,
                StartUtc:   createDto.StartUtc,
                EndUtc:     createDto.EndUtc,
                VenueName:  createDto.VenueName,
                Location:   new GeoPoint(createDto.Latitude, createDto.Longitude),
                Status:     EventStatus.Draft,
                RiskLevel:  EventRiskLevel.Low,
                Version:    1,
                ConfidenceScore: 0f,                     // Will be filled later by ingestion
                FlyerImageUrl: createDto.FlyerImageUrl,
                CreatedAtUtc: now,
                UpdatedAtUtc: now);


            return Task.FromResult(agg);
        }
    }
}

Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Reason
	Mapper must be pure – no DB, HTTP or file‑system calls.
	Guarantees deterministic unit tests.
	EventDto.Status is a string (enum name) to keep the front‑end type‑agnostic.
	Aligns with JSON conventions.
	FromCreateDtoAsync must set Version = 1, RiskLevel = Low, ConfidenceScore = 0 and Status = Draft – the later pipeline will update these.
	

	The mapper must never copy internal fields such as Version, RiskLevel, ConfidenceScore.
	Enforces the DTO boundary.
	Acceptance Criteria
* EventDto and CreateEventDto records compile and contain exactly the fields shown.
* IEventMapper exists with the two async methods.
* EventMapper implements the interface and performs the conversions shown, without any external calls.
* Unit‑test placeholders (WeUP.Tests/Events/EventMapperTests.cs) verify:
   1. A fully populated EventAggregate round‑trips to EventDto with correct values.
   2. FromCreateDtoAsync produces an aggregate with a fresh Guid, Version = 1, RiskLevel = Low, and timestamps set to UtcNow.
* DI registration: services.AddTransient<IEventMapper, EventMapper>();.

M4‑P18 — Implement Event Versioning and Update Semantics v1.0
markdown
# Context
Events may be edited after publishing (e.g., corrected title, venue, time).  The system must preserve **immutable history**: every change creates a new version of the aggregate while the previous version is stored for audit/rollback.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define an **EF‑Core version entity** (`EventVersionEntity`) that stores a snapshot of every version.  
2️⃣ Declare a **service contract** (`IEventVersioningService`) that:  
   - Retrieves the current aggregate.  
   - Persists a snapshot of the current state.  
   - Applies a pure update function and writes the new aggregate with an incremented version.  
3️⃣ Provide a **reference implementation** (`EventVersioningService`) that uses the existing `WeUpDbContext`.  
4️⃣ Add **optimistic concurrency** (row‑version) to the main `EventAggregateEntity`.  
5️⃣ Scaffold a **unit‑test** that asserts version increment, snapshot creation, and immutability.


# EF‑Core Entity (place under `WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Entities/EventVersionEntity.cs`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Entities/EventVersionEntity.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


/// <summary>
/// Immutable snapshot of an event at a specific version.
/// Stored for audit / rollback.  Append‑only – updates are prohibited
/// (see configuration below).
/// </summary>
public sealed class EventVersionEntity
{
    public Guid Id { get; set; }               // PK (different from EventId)
    public Guid EventId { get; set; }           // FK to the canonical aggregate
    public int Version { get; set; }           // Version number (1‑based)
    public string Title { get; set; } = default!;
    public DateTimeOffset StartUtc { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset? EndUtc { get; set; }
    public string VenueName { get; set; } = default!;
    public double Latitude { get; set; }
    public double Longitude { get; set; }
    public EventStatus Status { get; set; }
    public EventRiskLevel RiskLevel { get; set; }
    public float ConfidenceScore { get; set; }
    public string? FlyerImageUrl { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset SnapshotUtc { get; set; }   // When this version was captured
}


# Context
Events may be edited after publishing (e.g., corrected title, venue, time).  The system must preserve **immutable history**: every change creates a new version of the aggregate while the previous version is stored for audit/rollback.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define an **EF‑Core version entity** (`EventVersionEntity`) that stores a snapshot of every version.  
2️⃣ Declare a **service contract** (`IEventVersioningService`) that:  
   - Retrieves the current aggregate.  
   - Persists a snapshot of the current state.  
   - Applies a pure update function and writes the new aggregate with an incremented version.  
3️⃣ Provide a **reference implementation** (`EventVersioningService`) that uses the existing `WeUpDbContext`.

5️⃣ Scaffold a **unit‑test** that asserts version increment, snapshot creation, and immutability.

csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/EventVersionConfiguration.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Configurations;


public sealed class EventVersionConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<EventVersionEntity>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<EventVersionEntity> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("EventVersions");
        builder.HasKey(e => e.Id);
        builder.HasIndex(e => new { e.EventId, e.Version }).IsUnique();


        // Append‑only – disallow UPDATE/DELETE via EF.
        builder.Metadata.SetAfterSaveBehavior(PropertySaveBehavior.Throw);
    }
}

Service Contract (under WeUP.Domain/Events/IEventVersioningService.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Events/IEventVersioningService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Events
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles immutable updates to an EventAggregate:
    ///   * Creates a snapshot (EventVersionEntity) of the *current* state,
    ///   * Applies a pure update function,
    ///   * Persists the new aggregate with Version = old.Version + 1.
    /// All operations are performed in a single DB transaction.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IEventVersioningService
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Apply an update to the event identified by <paramref name="eventId"/>.
        /// The <paramref name="updater"/> receives the current aggregate and must
        /// return a **new** aggregate (no mutation of the input).  The method
        /// returns the freshly persisted aggregate.
        /// </summary>
        Task<EventAggregate> UpdateAsync(Guid eventId, Func<EventAggregate, EventAggregate> updater);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Domain/Events/IEventVersioningService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Events
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Handles immutable updates to an EventAggregate:
    ///   * Creates a snapshot (EventVersionEntity) of the *current* state,
    ///   * Applies a pure update function,
    ///   * Persists the new aggregate with Version = old.Version + 1.
    /// All operations are performed in a single DB transaction.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IEventVersioningService
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Apply an update to the event identified by <paramref name="eventId"/>.
        /// The <paramref name="updater"/> receives the current aggregate and must
        /// return a **new** aggregate (no mutation of the input).  The method
        /// returns the freshly persisted aggregate.
        /// </summary>
        Task<EventAggregate> UpdateAsync(Guid eventId, Func<EventAggregate, EventAggregate> updater);
    }
}


Reference Implementation (under WeUP.Application/Events/EventVersioningService.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Events/EventVersioningService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;
using WeUP.Domain.Events;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


namespace WeUP.Application.Events
{
    public sealed class EventVersioningService : IEventVersioningService
    {
        private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
        private readonly ILogger<EventVersioningService> _log;


        public EventVersioningService(WeUpDbContext db, ILogger<EventVersioningService> log)
        {
            _db = db;
            _log = log;
        }


        public async Task<EventAggregate> UpdateAsync(Guid eventId, Func<EventAggregate, EventAggregate> updater)
        {
            // ---------- 1️⃣ Load current aggregate ----------
            var curEntity = await _db.EventAggregates
                                      .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EventId == eventId)
                                      ?? throw new InvalidOperationException($"Event {eventId} not found.");


            var current = MapEntityToAggregate(curEntity);


            // ---------- 2️⃣ Persist snapshot ----------
            var snapshot = new EventVersionEntity
            {
                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
                EventId = current.EventId,
                Version = current.Version,
                Title = current.Title,
                StartUtc = current.StartUtc,
                EndUtc = current.EndUtc,
                VenueName = current.VenueName,
                Latitude = current.Location.Latitude,
                Longitude = current.Location.Longitude,
                Status = current.Status,
                RiskLevel = current.RiskLevel,
                ConfidenceScore = current.ConfidenceScore,
                FlyerImageUrl = current.FlyerImageUrl,
                SnapshotUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow
            };
            _db.EventVersions.Add(snapshot);


            // ---------- 3️⃣ Apply pure update ----------
            var updated = updater(current) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Updater returned null.");
            var newVersion = current.Version + 1;


            // ---------- 4️⃣ Write new aggregate ----------
            var updatedEntity = new EventAggregateEntity
            {
                EventId = updated.EventId,
                Title = updated.Title,
                StartUtc = updated.StartUtc,
                EndUtc = updated.EndUtc,
                VenueName = updated.VenueName,
                Latitude = updated.Location.Latitude,
                Longitude = updated.Location.Longitude,
                Status = updated.Status,
                RiskLevel = updated.RiskLevel,
                Version = newVersion,
                ConfidenceScore = updated.ConfidenceScore,
                FlyerImageUrl = updated.FlyerImageUrl,
                CreatedAtUtc = curEntity.CreatedAtUtc,
                UpdatedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                RowVersion = curEntity.RowVersion   // EF will enforce optimistic concurrency
            };


            // Replace the existing row (EF treats this as an UPDATE).
            _db.Entry(curEntity).CurrentValues.SetValues(updatedEntity);


            // ---------- 5️⃣ Commit atomically ----------
            await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


            _log.LogInformation(
                "Event {EventId} versioned: {OldVersion}->{NewVersion}",
                eventId, current.Version, newVersion);


            // Return the freshly persisted aggregate.
            return MapEntityToAggregate(updatedEntity);
        }


        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        // Mapping helpers – pure, deterministic.
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        private static EventAggregate MapEntityToAggregate(EventAggregateEntity e) =>
            new EventAggregate(
                EventId: e.EventId,
                Title: e.Title,
                StartUtc: e.StartUtc,
                EndUtc: e.EndUtc,
                VenueName: e.VenueName,
                Location: new GeoPoint(e.Latitude, e.Longitude),
                Status: e.Status,
                RiskLevel: e.RiskLevel,
                Version: e.Version,
                ConfidenceScore: e.ConfidenceScore,
                FlyerImageUrl: e.FlyerImageUrl,
                CreatedAtUtc: e.CreatedAtUtc,
                UpdatedAtUtc: e.UpdatedAtUtc);
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Events/EventVersioningService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events;
using WeUP.Domain.Events;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Entities;


namespace WeUP.Application.Events
{
    public sealed class EventVersioningService : IEventVersioningService
    {
        private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
        private readonly ILogger<EventVersioningService> _log;


        public EventVersioningService(WeUpDbContext db, ILogger<EventVersioningService> log)
        {
            _db = db;
            _log = log;
        }


        public async Task<EventAggregate> UpdateAsync(Guid eventId, Func<EventAggregate, EventAggregate> updater)
        {
            // ---------- 1️⃣ Load current aggregate ----------
            var curEntity = await _db.EventAggregates
                                      .FirstOrDefaultAsync(e => e.EventId == eventId)
                                      ?? throw new InvalidOperationException($"Event {eventId} not found.");


            var current = MapEntityToAggregate(curEntity);


            // ---------- 2️⃣ Persist snapshot ----------
            var snapshot = new EventVersionEntity
            {
                Id = Guid.NewGuid(),
                EventId = current.EventId,
                Version = current.Version,
                Title = current.Title,
                StartUtc = current.StartUtc,
                EndUtc = current.EndUtc,
                VenueName = current.VenueName,
                Latitude = current.Location.Latitude,
                Longitude = current.Location.Longitude,
                Status = current.Status,
                RiskLevel = current.RiskLevel,
                ConfidenceScore = current.ConfidenceScore,
                FlyerImageUrl = current.FlyerImageUrl,
                SnapshotUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow
            };
            _db.EventVersions.Add(snapshot);


            // ---------- 3️⃣ Apply pure update ----------
            var updated = updater(current) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Updater returned null.");
            var newVersion = current.Version + 1;


            // ---------- 4️⃣ Write new aggregate ----------
            var updatedEntity = new EventAggregateEntity
            {
                EventId = updated.EventId,
                Title = updated.Title,
                StartUtc = updated.StartUtc,
                EndUtc = updated.EndUtc,
                VenueName = updated.VenueName,
                Latitude = updated.Location.Latitude,
                Longitude = updated.Location.Longitude,
                Status = updated.Status,
                RiskLevel = updated.RiskLevel,
                Version = newVersion,
                ConfidenceScore = updated.ConfidenceScore,
                FlyerImageUrl = updated.FlyerImageUrl,
                CreatedAtUtc = curEntity.CreatedAtUtc,
                UpdatedAtUtc = DateTimeOffset.UtcNow,
                RowVersion = curEntity.RowVersion   // EF will enforce optimistic concurrency
            };


            // Replace the existing row (EF treats this as an UPDATE).
            _db.Entry(curEntity).CurrentValues.SetValues(updatedEntity);


            // ---------- 5️⃣ Commit atomically ----------
            await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


            _log.LogInformation(
                "Event {EventId} versioned: {OldVersion}->{NewVersion}",
                eventId, current.Version, newVersion);


            // Return the freshly persisted aggregate.
            return MapEntityToAggregate(updatedEntity);
        }


        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        // Mapping helpers – pure, deterministic.
        // -----------------------------------------------------------------
        private static EventAggregate MapEntityToAggregate(EventAggregateEntity e) =>
            new EventAggregate(
                EventId: e.EventId,
                Title: e.Title,
                StartUtc: e.StartUtc,
                EndUtc: e.EndUtc,
                VenueName: e.VenueName,
                Location: new GeoPoint(e.Latitude, e.Longitude),
                Status: e.Status,
                RiskLevel: e.RiskLevel,
                Version: e.Version,
                ConfidenceScore: e.ConfidenceScore,
                FlyerImageUrl: e.FlyerImageUrl,
                CreatedAtUtc: e.CreatedAtUtc,
                UpdatedAtUtc: e.UpdatedAtUtc);
    }
}


Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Reason
	The service must first store an immutable EventVersionEntity before applying any update.
	Guarantees a complete audit trail.
	The updater function must not mutate the incoming aggregate – it must return a brand‑new instance (enforced by a null‑check).
	Prevents accidental side‑effects.
	Version on the new aggregate must be exactly old.Version + 1.
	Enables deterministic version tracking.
	The EF configuration on EventAggregateEntity (already present elsewhere) must contain a [Timestamp] row‑version to protect concurrent updates.
	

	The entire operation runs inside a single SaveChangesAsync call → atomic.
	

	No external services are called – the service is pure persistence logic.
	

	Acceptance Criteria
* EventVersionEntity class and its EF configuration compile and enforce a unique (EventId, Version) index.
* IEventVersioningService interface exists with the UpdateAsync method.
* EventVersioningService implements the contract, creates a snapshot, applies the updater, increments the version, and saves atomically.
* Unit‑test WeUP.Tests/Events/EventVersioningServiceTests.cs verifies:
   1. After an update, a new row appears in EventVersions with the old version data.
   2. EventAggregate.Version is incremented by 1.
   3. Concurrency conflict (two updates with same original row‑version) raises DbUpdateConcurrencyException.
* DI registration: services.AddTransient<IEventVersioningService, EventVersioningService>();.

M4‑P19 — Enforce Schema Consistency and Contract Validation v1.0
markdown
# Context
Even with DTO‑to‑domain mapping, inbound payloads (create / update requests) can violate business rules (missing required fields, invalid latitude/longitude, start > end, etc.).  A deterministic validator must run **before** any persistence occurs and return a clear list of validation errors.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define a **validation result** record (`ValidationResult`).  
2️⃣ Create a **validator interface** for `EventDto` and `CreateEventDto`.  
3️⃣ Provide a **pure implementation** (`EventDtoValidator`) that checks:  
   - Required strings are non‑empty.  
   - Latitude ∈ [-90, 90], Longitude ∈ [-180, 180].  
   - `StartUtc` ≤ `EndUtc` (if `EndUtc` supplied).  
   - `Status` (for updates) is a valid enum name.  
4️⃣ Add a **pipeline helper** (`ValidateAndThrowAsync`) that the API controllers can call to short‑circuit with a 400 response.  
5️⃣ Scaffold a **unit‑test** that confirms each validation rule produces the expected error message.


# Contracts (place under `WeUP.Contracts/Events/Validation`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Events/Validation/ValidationResult.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Collections.Generic;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Events.Validation;


/// <summary>
/// Result of a DTO validation operation.
/// </summary>
public sealed record ValidationResult(
    bool IsValid,
    IReadOnlyList<string> Errors);

   - Required strings are non‑empty.  
   - Latitude ∈ [-90, 90], Longitude ∈ [-180, 180].  
   - `StartUtc` ≤ `EndUtc` (if `EndUtc` supplied).  
   - `Status` (for updates) is a valid enum name.  
4️⃣ Add a **pipeline helper** (`ValidateAndThrowAsync`) that the API controllers can call to short‑circuit with a 400 response.  
5️⃣ Scaffold a **unit‑test** that confirms each validation rule produces the expected error message.

using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Dto;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Validation;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Events.Validation
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Stateless validator for incoming event DTOs.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IEventValidator
    {
        Task<ValidationResult> ValidateCreateAsync(CreateEventDto createDto);
        Task<ValidationResult> ValidateUpdateAsync(EventDto updateDto);
    }
}


Implementation (place under WeUP.Application/Events/EventDtoValidator.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Events/EventDtoValidator.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Dto;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Validation;
using WeUP.Domain.Events.Validation;


namespace WeUP.Application.Events
{
    public sealed class EventDtoValidator : IEventValidator
    {
        public Task<ValidationResult> ValidateCreateAsync(CreateEventDto dto) =>
            ValidateAsync(dto.Title, dto.StartUtc, dto.EndUtc, dto.VenueName,
                         dto.Latitude, dto.Longitude, status: null);


        public Task<ValidationResult> ValidateUpdateAsync(EventDto dto) =>
            ValidateAsync(dto.Title, dto.StartUtc, dto.EndUtc, dto.VenueName,
                         dto.Latitude, dto.Longitude, status: dto.Status);


        private static Task<ValidationResult> ValidateAsync(
            string title,
            DateTimeOffset startUtc,
            DateTimeOffset? endUtc,
            string venueName,
            double latitude,
            double longitude,
            string? status)
        {
            var errors = new List<string>();


            // ----- Required strings -----
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title))
                errors.Add("Title is required and cannot be empty.");


            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(venueName))
                errors.Add("VenueName is required and cannot be empty.");


            // ----- Temporal logic -----
            if (startUtc == default)
                errors.Add("StartUtc must be a valid UTC timestamp.");


            if (endUtc.HasValue && endUtc <= startUtc)
                errors.Add("EndUtc, if supplied, must be later than StartUtc.");


            // ----- Geographic bounds -----
            if (latitude < -90 || latitude > 90)
                errors.Add($"Latitude {latitude} is out of range (must be between -90 and 90).");


            if (longitude < -180 || longitude > 180)
                errors.Add($"Longitude {longitude} is out of range (must be between -180 and 180).");


            // ----- Status (only for updates) -----
            if (status != null && !Enum.TryParse<EventStatus>(status, true, out _))
                errors.Add($"Status '{status}' is not a valid EventStatus value.");


            var result = new ValidationResult(
                IsValid: errors.Count == 0,
                Errors: errors);


            return Task.FromResult(result);
        }
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Events/EventDtoValidator.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Dto;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Validation;
using WeUP.Domain.Events.Validation;


namespace WeUP.Application.Events
{
    public sealed class EventDtoValidator : IEventValidator
    {
        public Task<ValidationResult> ValidateCreateAsync(CreateEventDto dto) =>
            ValidateAsync(dto.Title, dto.StartUtc, dto.EndUtc, dto.VenueName,
                         dto.Latitude, dto.Longitude, status: null);


        public Task<ValidationResult> ValidateUpdateAsync(EventDto dto) =>
            ValidateAsync(dto.Title, dto.StartUtc, dto.EndUtc, dto.VenueName,
                         dto.Latitude, dto.Longitude, status: dto.Status);


        private static Task<ValidationResult> ValidateAsync(
            string title,
            DateTimeOffset startUtc,
            DateTimeOffset? endUtc,
            string venueName,
            double latitude,
            double longitude,
            string? status)
        {
            var errors = new List<string>();


            // ----- Required strings -----
            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(title))
                errors.Add("Title is required and cannot be empty.");


            if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(venueName))
                errors.Add("VenueName is required and cannot be empty.");


            // ----- Temporal logic -----
            if (startUtc == default)
                errors.Add("StartUtc must be a valid UTC timestamp.");


            if (endUtc.HasValue && endUtc <= startUtc)
                errors.Add("EndUtc, if supplied, must be later than StartUtc.");


            // ----- Geographic bounds -----
            if (latitude < -90 || latitude > 90)
                errors.Add($"Latitude {latitude} is out of range (must be between -90 and 90).");


            if (longitude < -180 || longitude > 180)
                errors.Add($"Longitude {longitude} is out of range (must be between -180 and 180).");


            // ----- Status (only for updates) -----
            if (status != null && !Enum.TryParse<EventStatus>(status, true, out _))
                errors.Add($"Status '{status}' is not a valid EventStatus value.");


            var result = new ValidationResult(
                IsValid: errors.Count == 0,
                Errors: errors);


            return Task.FromResult(result);
        }
    }
}


Helper Extension (optional – placed in WeUP.Api/Extensions/ValidationExtensions.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Phase0_AIStudio/backend/WeUP.Api/Extensions/ValidationExtensions.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.AspNetCore.Mvc;
using WeUP.Contracts.Events.Validation;


namespace WeUP.Api.Extensions
{
    public static class ValidationExtensions
    {
        public static async Task<ActionResult> ValidateAndBadRequestAsync(
            this ControllerBase controller,
            ValidationResult validation)
        {
            if (validation.IsValid) return null!;
            var problem = new ValidationProblemDetails
            {
                Title = "One or more validation errors occurred.",
                Status = 400,
                Errors = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, string[]>
                {
                    { "ValidationErrors", validation.Errors.ToArray() }
                }
            };
            return controller.BadRequest(problem);
        }
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Phase0_AIStudio/backend/WeUP.Api/Extensions/ValidationExtensions.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------

namespace WeUP.Api.Extensions

    public static class ValidationExtensions
    {
        public static async Task<ActionResult> ValidateAndBadRequestAsync(
            this ControllerBase controller,
            ValidationResult validation)
        {
            if (validation.IsValid) return null!;
            var problem = new ValidationProblemDetails
            {
                Title = "One or more validation errors occurred.",
                Status = 400,
                Errors = new System.Collections.Generic.Dictionary<string, string[]>
                {
                    { "ValidationErrors", validation.Errors.ToArray() }
                }
            };
            return controller.BadRequest(problem);
        }
    }

Rules & Constraints
Rule
	Reason
	Validator must be pure – only synchronous checks, no I/O.
	Guarantees deterministic unit tests.
	All error messages are human‑readable and must be added to ValidationResult.Errors.
	Enables the API to return a clear 400 payload.
	When status is supplied (update DTO) it must be parsable to EventStatus.
	Prevents invalid enum values from reaching the domain.
	Latitude / Longitude validation uses the exact bounds defined in the domain contract.
	Guarantees consistency across the stack.
	Acceptance Criteria
* ValidationResult, IEventValidator, and EventDtoValidator compile.
* EventDtoValidator checks all rules listed and returns a ValidationResult with IsValid = false when any rule fails.
* Unit‑test skeleton WeUP.Tests/Events/EventDtoValidatorTests.cs verifies each rule: missing title, invalid latitude, start > end, invalid status, etc.
* Controllers can call await this.ValidateAndBadRequestAsync(result) (optional helper).
* DI registration: services.AddTransient<IEventValidator, EventDtoValidator>();.

M4‑P20 — Track Merge Lineage and Event Evolution v1.0
markdown
# Context
When duplicate events are merged (M2‑P09), every field change must leave an **immutable provenance trail** so auditors can reconstruct *who* contributed *what* and *when*.  The lineage is also needed by the UI for “event history” views.


# Objective
1️⃣ Define a **merge‑lineage record** (`MergeHistoryEntry`).  
2️⃣ Define a **service contract** (`IMergeHistoryService`) to record and query lineage.  
3️⃣ Create an **EF‑Core entity** (`MergeHistoryEntity`) with append‑only semantics.  
4️⃣ Implement a **service** (`MergeHistoryService`) that writes immutable rows and returns ordered history.  
5️⃣ Add a **method** on `IEventDetailService` (or a new query service) that returns the lineage together with the event detail (the DTO already contains a `Provenance` collection from M2‑P10 – this is the same data, but we expose it via a dedicated service for clarity).  
6️⃣ Provide a **unit‑test** that verifies immutability (duplicate IDs are ignored) and correct ordering.


# Contracts (under `WeUP.Contracts/Resolution`)


```csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Resolution/MergeHistoryEntry.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;


namespace WeUP.Contracts.Resolution;


/// <summary>
/// One immutable record describing a field‑level change performed during a merge operation.
/// </summary>
public sealed record MergeHistoryEntry(
    Guid Id,                       // New GUID per entry
    Guid CanonicalEventId,         // Event that was mutated
    Guid CandidateEventId,         // Source of the new data
    string FieldName,              // e.g. "Title", "StartUtc", "VenueName"
    string? OldValue,             // Null if the field did not exist before
    string? NewValue,             // New value after the merge
    DateTimeOffset ChangedAtUtc,  // When the merge was applied
    string ChangedBy,             // Usually the system user "MergeEngine"
    string Reason);               // Human‑readable justification (e.g., "Auto‑merge high similarity")

csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Contracts/Resolution/IMergeHistoryService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using WeUP.Contracts.Resolution;


namespace WeUP.Domain.Resolution
{
    /// <summary>
    /// Service responsible for persisting immutable merge‑history entries
    /// and retrieving the full lineage for a given event.
    /// </summary>
    public interface IMergeHistoryService
    {
        /// <summary>
        /// Record a new merge‑history entry.  Must be append‑only.
        /// </summary>
        Task RecordAsync(MergeHistoryEntry entry);


        /// <summary>
        /// Retrieve the complete merge lineage for <paramref name="eventId"/> ordered
        /// by <c>ChangedAtUtc</c> ascending.
        /// </summary>
        Task<IReadOnlyList<MergeHistoryEntry>> GetLineageAsync(Guid eventId);
    }
}

using System;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Resolution;


/// <summary>
/// Append‑only table that stores every field‑level change performed by the merge planner.
/// </summary>
public sealed class MergeHistoryEntity
{
    public Guid Id { get; set; }
    public Guid CanonicalEventId { get; set; }
    public Guid CandidateEventId { get; set; }
    public string FieldName { get; set; } = default!;
    public string? OldValue { get; set; }
    public string? NewValue { get; set; }
    public DateTimeOffset ChangedAtUtc { get; set; }
    public string ChangedBy { get; set; } = default!;
    public string Reason { get; set; } = default!;


    // Append‑only – prevent updates/deletes via EF.
    [Timestamp]
    public byte[] RowVersion { get; set; } = null!;
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Infrastructure/Persistence/Configurations/MergeHistoryConfiguration.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore.Metadata.Builders;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Resolution;


namespace WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence.Configurations;


public sealed class MergeHistoryConfiguration : IEntityTypeConfiguration<MergeHistoryEntity>
{
    public void Configure(EntityTypeBuilder<MergeHistoryEntity> builder)
    {
        builder.ToTable("MergeHistory");
        builder.HasKey(e => e.Id);
        builder.Property(e => e.FieldName).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(e => e.ChangedBy).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(100);
        builder.Property(e => e.Reason).IsRequired().HasMaxLength(500);


        // Append‑only semantics – throw on accidental update/delete.
        builder.Metadata.SetAfterSaveBehavior(PropertySaveBehavior.Throw);
    }
}

Service Implementation (under WeUP.Application/Resolution/MergeHistoryService.cs)
csharp
// ------------------------------------------------------------
// File: WeUP.Application/Resolution/MergeHistoryService.cs
// ------------------------------------------------------------
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using System.Linq;
using System.Threading.Tasks;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;
using Microsoft.Extensions.Logging;
using WeUP.Contracts.Resolution;
using WeUP.Domain.Resolution;
using WeUP.Infrastructure.Persistence;


namespace WeUP.Application.Resolution
{
    public sealed class MergeHistoryService : IMergeHistoryService
    {
        private readonly WeUpDbContext _db;
        private readonly ILogger<MergeHistoryService> _log;


        public MergeHistoryService(WeUpDbContext db, ILogger<MergeHistoryService> log)
        {
            _db = db;
            _log = log;
        }


        public async Task RecordAsync(MergeHistoryEntry entry)
        {
            // Guard against duplicate IDs – the GUID should be unique per entry.
            var exists = await _db.MergeHistory.AnyAsync(e => e.Id == entry.Id);
            if (exists)
            {
                _log.LogWarning("Duplicate merge history entry ignored: {Id}", entry.Id);
                return;
            }


            var entity = new MergeHistoryEntity
            {
                Id = entry.Id,
                CanonicalEventId = entry.CanonicalEventId,
                CandidateEventId = entry.CandidateEventId,
                FieldName = entry.FieldName,
                OldValue = entry.OldValue,
                NewValue = entry.NewValue,
                ChangedAtUtc = entry.ChangedAtUtc,
                ChangedBy = entry.ChangedBy,
                Reason = entry.Reason
            };


            _db.MergeHistory.Add(entity);
            await _db.SaveChangesAsync();


            _log.LogInformation(
                "Merge history recorded: Event {EventId}, Field {Field}, {Old}→{New}",
                entry.CanonicalEventId, entry.FieldName, entry.OldValue, entry.NewValue);
        }


        public async Task<IReadOnlyList<MergeHistoryEntry>> GetLineageAsync(Guid eventId)
        {
            var rows = await _db.MergeHistory
                .Where(e => e.CanonicalEventId == eventId)
                .OrderBy(e => e.ChangedAtUtc)
                .ToListAsync();


            var result = rows.Select(e => new MergeHistoryEntry(
                Id: e.Id,
                CanonicalEventId: e.CanonicalEventId,
                CandidateEventId: e.CandidateEventId,
                FieldName: e.FieldName,
                OldValue: e.OldValue,
                NewValue: e.NewValue,
                ChangedAtUtc: e.ChangedAtUtc,
                ChangedBy: e.ChangedBy,
                Reason: e.Reason)).ToArray();


            return result;
        }
    }
}


// ------------------------------------------------------------
